# TCP Facts 2026-11-23

2177 checked facts about T Cloud Public (TCP). One line per fact, grouped by topic. Read the comment at the top before answering.

## ecs (244)

- Each ECS flavor exposes info:cpu:name in os_extra_specs, giving CPU microarchitecture and clock, for example Intel Cascade Lake 2.6GHz. The newest s9 and c9 families return masked values such as Intel CPU N.NGHz or General Purpose Processor. (KB-232P, V4 kept live, checked 2026-07-19)
- The service description contracts an availability SLA for ECS, EVS and OBS. Periods in which at least one AZ of the region stays up are excluded from the downtime calculation. (KB-23F7, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-10-14)
- The ECS flavor listing at /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/flavors in eu-de returns os_extra_specs per flavor. cond:operation:az holds the state per AZ, for example eu-de-01(sellout),eu-de-03(normal), with the values normal, sellout and abandon. The per-AZ value overrides the top-level cond:operation:status, so status abandon with eu-de-01(normal) still means orderable in eu-de-01. (KB-25LL, V4 kept live, checked 2026-06-11)
- After POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/delete with delete_volume true for two ECS (m9.2xlarge.8 and pi5e.2xlarge.4 with SSD root volumes created in the create call), GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/detail and GET /v2/{project_id}/cloudvolumes/detail listed neither the servers nor their root volumes about two minutes later, so a cleanup can be verified from these two list calls alone without waiting on the delete job. (KB-2INS, V3 live, checked 2026-10-21)
- A Windows ECS created with adminPass instead of key_name demands a password change at first logon, and with NLA active RDP then fails with 0x1207. With key_name, the password is read from GET /v2.1/{project_id}/servers/{id}/os-server-password, base64-decoded and decrypted with the private key. (KB-2K2K, V3 live, checked 2026-06-11)
- The default ECS quotas in eu-de are maxTotalInstances 100, maxTotalCores 800 and maxTotalRAMSize 2500000 MB, about 2441 GiB. They are read with GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/limits and GET /v1/{project_id}/quotas. (KB-2QQ4, V4 kept live, checked 2026-08-25)
- os-getConsoleOutput returns only the tail of the serial console log when a length is passed, and the log can lag behind the finish of cloud-init. Requesting the full output without a length parameter returns earlier lines that the tail is missing. (KB-2SFK, V3 live, checked 2026-07-17)
- os-getConsoleOutput returns an empty output for the public Ubuntu 24.04 UEFI image on c9.2xlarge.2 in eu-de-01, as it does for the Meraki vMX image on c9: the c9 family gives no serial console log for Linux guests either, so a probe on c9 needs another signal, such as a self-halt visible as SHUTOFF in the server status. (KB-2VOR, V3 live, checked 2026-10-20)
- Starting a stopped standby ECS instance took 11 s with a Debian guest and 23 s with a Windows Server 2022 guest. (KB-2XII, V3 live, checked 2026-09-02)
- In eu-de a Windows Server 2012 R2 image (Hyper-V Generation 1 VHDX, all updates, virtio-win 0.1.215, imported as BIOS) on an ECS of flavor c4.large.4 comes back after a SOFT reboot through POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/action and after os-stop SOFT plus os-start: each time the new boot time is reported within about 3 minutes, with viostor and netkvm running and the same DHCP address. (KB-32JM, V3 live, checked 2026-11-20)
- The storage optimized ECS families i3 and i3m carry local NVMe disks: i3 one to eight disks of 3,200 GiB, i3m one to seven of 1,600 GiB. The service description revision 17.08.2026 offers both only in EU-NL, and on 2026-09-25 the flavor list of eu-nl carried 10 i3 and 6 i3m flavors while eu-de carried none. Section 6.1.1 says the i3 and d2 flavors and all dedicated host types are also charged in the shutdown state. (KB-3744, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- For an appliance image that carries no cloud-init, the first-boot administrator password is set over the ECS VNC remote-login console (os-getVNCConsole, novnc) instead of the adminPass field of the ECS create body. (KB-37RS, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-02-04)
- os-getConsoleOutput returns HTTP 200 with empty output for an ECS that does not actively write to ttyS0, which is typical for imported images. The same call returns text for a guest that writes to ttyS0 from user_data. (KB-3ECJ, V3 live, checked 2026-06-12)
- The price API lists the ECS flavor families under six service names: ecs (s2, s3, s7n, s9), ecsflex (x1, x1e, x1m, x2, x2e, x2m, x2ne), ecsnoc (c4, c4ne, c7n, c7t, c9), memo (m4, m7n, m9), lms (e3) and gpu (the GPU families). A search under sn=ecs alone misses most families, and m3 appears only among the MRS node prices. (KB-3KHR, V3 live, checked 2026-09-24)
- GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/detail with a read-only key that lacks the ECS role returns HTTP 403 with code Ecs.0110, do not have the required roles, so that error says nothing about the servers in the project. (KB-3KRS, V3 live, checked 2026-11-05)
- ECS auto-recovery appears in CTS as the trace names addAutoRecovery and deleteAutoRecovery. These record the configuration change, not a recovery event. (KB-3RWJ, V3 live, checked 2026-08-26)
- On 2026-09-25 every s9 size (21 sizes, s9.medium.1 to s9.16xlarge.4) read normal in cond:operation:az for eu-de-01, eu-de-02 and eu-de-03. (KB-3TBJ, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- A resize from s9 back to an older general-purpose family is refused with Ecs.0804, current flavor can not resize to flavor, so the rollback path is a restore from snapshot or backup. (KB-3XVN, V3 live, checked 2026-07-21)
- For an ECS on c9.2xlarge.2 in eu-de the host still records the guest serial port (the charserial log of qemu-kvm holds the U-Boot lines of the Meraki vMX), while os-getConsoleOutput returns no lines for it. (KB-45TV, V3 live, checked 2026-10-21)
- An ECS resize keeps the server in its original availability zone, so the target flavor has to have capacity in that same AZ. (KB-47AN, V3 live, checked 2026-07-21)
- A Linux ECS started from an Ubuntu public image has the default login user ubuntu and is set up for key-pair SSH login. (KB-47ME, V3 live, checked 2026-06-15)
- POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/delete on the ECS endpoint takes the fields delete_publicip and delete_volume. With delete_publicip false and delete_volume true the server, its system disk and its data disks are deleted, and the elastic IP stays in the account as unbound. (KB-4BMR, V3 live, checked 2026-06-17)
- An ECS create on c7n.large.2 in eu-de ignores hw:passthrough true in root_volume: the system disk still attaches as a virtio-blk disk (/dev/vda, bus virtio). (KB-4KZN, V3 live, checked 2026-10-01)
- GET /v1/{project_id}/os-keypairs on the ECS endpoint in eu-de answers HTTP 404. (KB-4NJ5, V4 kept live, checked 2026-11-03)
- An ECS create on c7n.large.2 in eu-de from an image with hw_disk_bus scsi and hw_scsi_model virtio-scsi fails: the job ends in Ecs.0204 with 'server is error, code is 0107012' and TCP rolls the server back. (KB-4XFL, V3 live, checked 2026-10-01)
- An ECS on c9.2xlarge.2 in eu-de created through the native v2.1 API from an image with hw_disk_bus ide, plus a blank block_device_mapping_v2 entry with device_type cdrom and disk_bus ide, reaches ACTIVE in about 30 s. The block_device API shows the system disk as /dev/hda (boot index 0) and the CD-ROM as /dev/hdb (boot index -1). (KB-5LOE, V3 live, checked 2026-10-21)
- The ECS batch delete call POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/delete takes the flags delete_volume and delete_publicip. With delete_publicip set to false the attached EIP stays allocated and is released separately with DELETE /v1/{project_id}/publicips/{eip_id}. (KB-5SA7, V3 live, checked 2026-07-09)
- A moved Windows domain member that cannot reach a domain controller on TCP lands in the Windows firewall public profile, which blocks inbound RDP even when the security group allows it; when group policy manages the firewall, netsh advfirewall show currentprofile reports local firewall rules as not applicable (GPO store only). (KB-5TJU, V3 live, checked 2026-11-13)
- An ECS create in eu-de from an image with hw_disk_bus sata fails on c7n.large.2, c9.large.2, s9.large.2 and s7n.large.2: the job ends in Ecs.0204 with 'server is error, code is 0300000' and TCP rolls the server back. (KB-5YJ7, V3 live, checked 2026-10-01)
- The web console limits a pasted public key to 1024 characters when a key pair is imported. The key pair API accepts a key longer than that. (KB-62HO, V3 live, checked 2026-02-04)
- An s3.medium.1 ECS from Standard_Ubuntu_24.04_amd64_bios_latest shows virtio network, virtio block (system disk vda), virtio console and virtio memory balloon devices on PCI, while lsmod lists no virtio module (the drivers are built into the kernel). (KB-66D7, V3 live, checked 2026-11-20)
- A root volume created inline with an ECS carries delete_on_termination true, which overrides delete_volume false at teardown and removes the disk. The current value shows in os-extended-volumes:volumes_attached[].delete_on_termination on the server. (KB-6C7X, V3 live, checked 2026-07-29)
- Opening the noVNC console for an ECS re-imaged to Windows, changing the password at the first-logon prompt and then connecting by RDP completes the login. (KB-6FEI, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-02-04)
- An ECS on c7n.large.2 in eu-de that boots from a UEFI image runs OVMF on the QEMU machine type pc-i440fx-4.1, with its system disk on virtio-blk. (KB-6KHR, V3 live, checked 2026-10-01)
- A SOFT os-stop of a g7v.2xlarge.8 Windows Server 2022 ECS in eu-de-01 through POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/action returned a job at once; the job reached SUCCESS and the server SHUTOFF 75 seconds later, and the server kept its flavor g7v.2xlarge.8 in the detail while stopped. (KB-6LDU, V3 live, checked 2026-11-18)
- Upstream OpenStack Nova accepts sata as a disk bus for the libvirt virt types kvm and qemu (SUPPORTED_DEVICE_BUSES lists virtio, scsi, ide, usb, fdc and sata), so hw_disk_bus=sata is a standard Nova option that TCP refuses with code 0300000. (KB-6MAQ, V1 docs, checked 2026-10-09)
- Flavor sellability has to be read from the per-AZ field cond:operation:az. The global cond:operation:status can report abandon for a flavor that is still sellable in an availability zone. (KB-6PLG, V4 kept live, checked 2026-06-12)
- An ECS create can return error Ecs.0319 with the message "capacity not sufficient", which reports a stock shortage of that flavor. A flavor listed with os_extra_specs["cond:operation:az"] = normal is orderable and can still return Ecs.0319. (KB-6VPI, V3 live, checked 2026-08-18)
- The ECS v1 create accepts a personality entry and creates the server, but on Standard_Ubuntu_24.04_amd64_bios_latest the file was not written into the guest, while cloud-init user_data of the same request ran; the ECS API reference does not document personality. (KB-76G4, V3 live, checked 2026-11-20)
- An ECS can read its own server id from the instance metadata service at /openstack/latest/meta_data.json. That id is what ECS API calls need to address the instance itself. (KB-7AIC, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-05-01)
- In the console the same setting is the Source/Destination Check toggle, reached on the ECS detail page under the Network Interfaces tab by opening the NIC's IP details. (KB-7HLP, V1 docs, checked 2026-07-23)
- An ECS create issued while the subnet is still in state BUILD fails. Polling the VPC to status OK and the subnet to status ACTIVE first makes the same create succeed. (KB-7LLJ, V3 live, checked 2026-08-18)
- A public Ubuntu 24.04 BIOS image on s3.medium.2 runs a user_data script that writes to /dev/ttyS0, and os-getConsoleOutput returns those lines, so a probe ECS in the same subnet can report checks through the API without an elastic IP. (KB-7VBC, V3 live, checked 2026-11-03)
- A Hyper-V Generation 2 VM on a host that runs on battery sees a Microsoft Hyper-V Virtual Battery (BatteryStatus 1); a scheduled task registered with default settings (DisallowStartIfOnBatteries True) then does not start on Start-ScheduledTask, while Get-ScheduledTaskInfo still shows LastTaskResult 0. On AC power the same task runs. New-ScheduledTaskSettingsSet -AllowStartIfOnBatteries -DontStopIfGoingOnBatteries avoids it. (KB-7W5A, V3 live, checked 2026-11-12)
- The flavor extra spec cond:operation:status reads abandon on c9.2xlarge.2, m9.2xlarge.8 and pi5e.2xlarge.4 in eu-de while cond:operation:az reads normal for their zones, and POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers creates and boots them without error. The abandon value of that key is not an availability statement; cond:operation:az is. (KB-A4DH, V4 kept live, checked 2026-10-21)
- The flavor cond:image support tags __support_kvm and __support_kvm_hi1822_hiovs are enforced by the console flavor picker only. With the hi1822 tag stripped from a private image, an s9 create and a resize from s3 to s9 both returned HTTP 200 and the instance booted ACTIVE. (KB-A4NI, V3 live, checked 2026-07-20)
- In eu-de a Windows Server 2012 R2 image (Hyper-V Generation 1 VHDX, all updates, virtio-win 0.1.215, imported as BIOS) on an ECS of flavor c4.large.4 comes back after a SOFT reboot through POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/action and after os-stop SOFT plus os-start: each time the new boot time is reported within about 3 minutes, with viostor and netkvm running and the same DHCP address. (KB-A5DZ, V3 live, checked 2026-11-20)
- The ECS api-ref documents the extra spec ecs:generation. The digit in a flavor name gives the generation, for example s3 is general computing III. (KB-A7FI, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-02)
- When an ECS is created with POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers, as after an image import or from a full-ECS image, an empty security_groups binds the default security group, an empty ip_address takes a free address of the subnet and a missing availability_zone lets the system pick the AZ. These three settings come from the create call, not from the image. (KB-AARL, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- GET /v2.1/{project_id}/servers/{id}/os-server-password returns an empty body, not an error, until Cloudbase-Init finishes the first boot of a key-pair Windows ECS. The base64 RSA ciphertext then appears and is decrypted with the key-pair private key. (KB-AEHL, V3 live, checked 2026-09-25)
- A g7v.2xlarge.8 Windows ECS in eu-de-01 that had been stopped for 3 hours 20 minutes started again without a capacity error: the os-start job reached SUCCESS 20 seconds after the call and the server was ACTIVE; a SOFT stop after 10 minutes reached SHUTOFF 41 seconds after the call. (KB-AETX, V3 live, checked 2026-11-18)
- POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/delete with delete_volume true and delete_publicip false removes an ECS with its data disks and leaves the EIP unbound. PUT /v1/{project_id}/publicips/{id} with a publicip.port_id taken from /cloudservers/{server_id}/os-interface rebinds that EIP to another server. (KB-AJER, V3 live, checked 2026-06-17)
- A Windows ECS created from an image registered with hw_firmware_type bios whose disk is a Hyper-V Generation 2 (GPT, UEFI) disk shows on the console 'Booting from Hard Disk...', then iPXE network boot over virtio-net and 'No bootable device'; the NIC still gets its DHCP address, so the disk lacks a BIOS boot loader and the image firmware has to be uefi. (KB-ALZ5, V3 live, checked 2026-11-13)
- A Debian 10.13 server moved from a Hyper-V Generation 1 VM (VHDX, initramfs with MODULES=most) to TCP boots on s7n.large.2 from a BIOS image (os_version Debian GNU/Linux 10.13.0 64bit) with its disk as virtio_blk vda, but the NIC is renamed from eth0 to ens3, so an /etc/network/interfaces with only "allow-hotplug eth0" leaves it down without an address. Adding "allow-hotplug ens3" and "iface ens3 inet dhcp" before the export keeps eth0 working on Hyper-V and gives the ECS its address, route and DNS on TCP. (KB-AMEW, V3 live, checked 2026-11-18)
- A new TCP Auto Scaling group is created in status PAUSED and needs the action resume; with a private Windows image on g7v.2xlarge.8 the first instance was INSERVICE 60 s after desire_instance_number was set to 1, and a g7v from a Sysprepped private image drew its vGPU lease like one from the public image. (KB-ANXL, V3 live, checked 2026-11-12)
- When an ECS is created from a full-ECS image, the data disks of the image are restored without being listed in data_volumes, and each data_volumes entry without extendparam.snapshotId adds a new disk. Per the API reference, an entry that carries the snapshotId of an image data disk changes that disk instead of adding one. (KB-AZ3P, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- On 2026-10-04 in eu-de the A40 flavor g7.12xlarge.8 (1 x A40 48G) read normal in eu-de-01 and eu-de-03, g7.24xlarge.8 (2 x A40) normal in eu-de-03 and sellout in eu-de-01, and the vGPU sizes g7v.2xlarge.8 and g7v.4xlarge.8 normal in eu-de-01. A normal zone state is a catalog state, not a stock guarantee. (KB-B4YD, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-10-04)
- The source and destination check and the EIP binding are properties of the port, not of the guest, so a rebuilt instance needs both applied again on its new ports, the EIP bound by port_id. (KB-B7RT, V3 live, checked 2026-07-29)
- POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/{id}/resize is asynchronous: it returns a job_id and the instance goes to status RESIZE. A start issued before GET /v1/{project_id}/jobs/{job_id} reports SUCCESS leaves the instance on its old flavor. A refused resize returns no job_id. (KB-BACI, V3 live, checked 2026-07-21)
- In the eu-de ECS flavor catalog, os_extra_specs["cond:operation:az"] carries per-AZ orderability with the values normal, sellout and abandon. The region-wide os_extra_specs["cond:operation:status"] reads abandon on 365 of 372 flavors, 327 of them normal in some AZ, so it does not separate orderable flavors (2026-09-25). (KB-BECR, V3 live, checked 2026-09-25)
- The private key material of a key pair created through the API is returned once, in the body of the create response. The key pair therefore has to exist before the ECS create that references it. (KB-BFKU, V3 live, checked 2026-08-18)
- The service description (revision 09.10.2026) lists physical Elastic Cloud Server flavors c9.144xl.2, c9.144xl.4 and m9.144xl.8 with 384 vCPU (c9.144xl.4 with 1536 GiB RAM), against 192 vCPU for the largest c9 and m9 virtual flavors and 208 for the e3 large-memory family. They are expected available from 01.10.2026 and offered on request only. (KB-BJGL, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-10-14)
- A c9.2xlarge.2 ECS in eu-de-01 gets no VMX (Intel VT-x) exposed to the guest: on Ubuntu 24.04 UEFI the kvm_intel module gives no /dev/kvm, so nested virtualization is not available on this flavor. Windows features that need the Hyper-V hypervisor inside the guest (VBS, Credential Guard, HVCI, WSL2, Windows Sandbox) cannot start on it. (KB-BJTC, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-10-20)
- An Ubuntu ECS without EIP and without NAT gateway in eu-de reaches the TCP apt mirror and OBS but not the public internet: a download from download.microsoft.com fails, so an install ISO must come in through OBS or a machine with internet. (KB-BKXF, V3 live, checked 2026-11-16)
- In the TCP service description price tables a c9 ECS flavor carries a higher open-Linux hourly rate than the s9 flavor of the same size. The checked pair was c9.large.2 against s9.large.2. (KB-BOS5, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-10-14)
- cond:operation:az with value normal means a flavor is orderable, not that stock exists. An ECS create or resize can return Ecs.0319, capacity of flavor not sufficient, while every AZ still advertises normal; s9 stock was briefly exhausted in eu-de-01, eu-de-02 and eu-de-03 at the same time. (KB-BUI2, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-07-21)
- The console Get Password section and the API GET /v2.1/{project_id}/servers/{server_id}/os-server-password apply to instances created with a key pair, where Cloudbase-Init generates a random password and encrypts it with the public key. An instance created with adminPass shows no Get Password section. (KB-CBPS, V3 live, checked 2026-06-10)
- On 2026-11-10 the eu-de region project of a TCP test tenant had used all 10 of its publicIp quota and 9 of 10 VPCs, while ECS stood at 14 of 100 instances and 87 of 800 cores, and its IAM account used 16 of 20 user groups and 5 of 10 subprojects in eu-de. (KB-CCJD, V3 live, checked 2026-11-11)
- os-getConsoleOutput on an ECS in eu-de that is still in BUILD (task_state spawning) answers HTTP 404 itemNotFound with 'Instance instance-... could not be found'. A few seconds later, still in spawning, the same call returns HTTP 200 with an empty output, and the boot lines follow once the server is ACTIVE. (KB-CF4H, V3 live, checked 2026-10-07)
- In TCP Auto Scaling an instance removed with action REMOVE and instance_delete yes while an INSTANCE_TERMINATING lifecycle hook exists waits in REMOVING_WAIT (desired already lowered by one) and stays there until PUT .../scaling_instance_hook/{group_id}/callback with CONTINUE; the ECS was deleted 15 s after the callback, which gives a drain window for session hosts. (KB-COPJ, V3 live, checked 2026-11-12)
- An ECS is created with POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers, which returns job_id and serverIds; the ECS jobs endpoint is polled until the server is ACTIVE. In the nics array the field subnet_id is the VPC v1 subnet id, and root_volume size must be at least the image min_disk. (KB-CYMN, V3 live, checked 2026-07-20)
- Console output timing depends on the host generation. c9 hosts wrap the lines in qemu-kvm charserial0 log prefixes and can lag minutes behind the guest, leaving the console empty long after boot, while s7n hosts deliver clean lines quickly. (KB-CZPT, V3 live, checked 2026-06-11)
- An m9.2xlarge.8 ECS in eu-de-01 gets no VMX (Intel VT-x) exposed to the guest: the kvm_intel module gives no /dev/kvm on Ubuntu 24.04 UEFI, so nested virtualization is off on the m9 family at 8 vCPU as on c9, and VBS or Credential Guard cannot start in a Windows guest on it despite the TPM and Secure Boot flags. (KB-DUTE, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-10-21)
- The Swiss T Cloud Public (eu-ch2) differs from eu-de per the Cloud Create guide: only s3 flavors (s3.medium.1 and the like), two availability zones eu-ch2a and eu-ch2b, EVS volume types SAS and SSD only, DNS private zones only (no public zones) and NAT gateway specifications of its own. Its documentation lives on a Swiss documentation portal of its own. (KB-DVB7, V1 docs, checked 2026-10-08)
- Attaching an EVS volume to a running ECS goes through the ECS API as POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/{server_id}/attachvolume. The call is asynchronous: it returns a job that has to be polled on the ECS jobs endpoint until the job state reads SUCCESS. (KB-DWRX, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-05-01)
- GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/flavors with availability_zone=eu-de-01 returned 326 flavors, 323 of them carrying os_extra_specs cond:operation:status abandon, among them s2 and s3 families that were orderable at that time. (KB-DXPP, V3 live, checked 2026-08-26)
- The system disk of a new ECS must be at least the min_disk of its source image. Private images with preinstalled software carried min_disk 200 GB, so a 120 GB system disk required a fresh public image instead. (KB-E6CH, V3 live, checked 2026-06-17)
- In eu-de right after the batch delete job of POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/delete reads SUCCESS, GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/{server_id} answers HTTP 200 with status DELETED and a terminated_at time, while the server has already left the detail list. A deletion check reads the status field. (KB-EBK4, V3 live, checked 2026-11-19)
- ECS quota increases go through the service hotline or email, per the ECS documentation on quota adjustment, so a large estate needs the request filed well before deployment. (KB-EOFG, V1 docs, checked 2026-08-25)
- T Cloud Public (TCP) provides virtual machines through Elastic Cloud Server (ECS) with Microsoft Windows Server and Linux operating systems: the public images include Windows Server, SUSE Linux Enterprise Server, Oracle Linux and Red Hat Enterprise Linux, and customers can also bring their own private images. Windows Server 2025 and Ubuntu, RHEL and other public images were listed live in eu-de on 2026-09-25 (KB-M3JR, KB-GP36). (KB-EPEA, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-10-14)
- A pi5e.2xlarge.4 ECS (one NVIDIA L4, Intel Ice Lake 6348 host) in eu-de-01 gets no VMX exposed to the guest: no /dev/kvm on the Ubuntu 24.04 GPU image, so nested virtualization is off on the pi5e GPU family too, the only family the TPM docs page names, and VBS or Credential Guard cannot start in a Windows guest on it. (KB-EPOM, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-10-21)
- In TCP Auto Scaling, PUT on the scaling group with desire_instance_number below the number of protected instances is refused with AS.2010 'The expected number cannot be less than the number of protected instances', so instance protection blocks a scale-in at the API before any removal policy applies. (KB-F24E, V3 live, checked 2026-11-12)
- PUT /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/{server_id}/os-reset-password on the ECS endpoint in eu-de returns 404 with error code APIGW.0101, The API does not exist or has not been published in the environment. The one-click Windows password reset of the upstream API is not published on T Cloud Public (TCP). (KB-F25K, V3 live, checked 2026-08-24)
- os-getConsoleOutput returned an empty output for public Ubuntu 24.04 guests on s9.large.2 in eu-de, while c7n.large.2 guests returned their kernel log within a minute. (KB-F27E, V3 live, checked 2026-10-01)
- A noVNC console URL for an ECS is returned by POST /v2.1/{project_id}/servers/{server_id}/action with the body {"os-getVNCConsole": {"type": "novnc"}}, on the host console.otc.t-systems.com (2026-09-25). A test in February found the URL valid for about five minutes. The ECS remote_console paths under v1 and v1.1 return HTTP 404. (KB-F6JF, V3 live, checked 2026-09-25)
- On 2026-11-13 GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/flavors?availability_zone=eu-de-01 listed m7n.xlarge.8 (4 vCPU, 32 GB) and m9.xlarge.8 next to s7n.xlarge.4 and s7n.2xlarge.4, so a 4 vCPU / 32 GB server has an exact flavor in eu-de-01. (KB-FI3C, V3 live, checked 2026-11-13)
- The port_id of each NIC is read from the ECS server detail response under addresses, keyed by vpc id, in the element field OS-EXT-IPS:port_id. The array order follows the order of the nics array at creation, so index 0 is port1. (KB-FNJH, V3 live, checked 2026-07-23)
- The U-Boot of the Meraki vMX 26.1.4 KVM image sets stdin=serial and stdout=serial,vidconsole with bootdelay -2. Its prompt shows on the VNC console but takes keyboard input only from the serial port, so typing in noVNC on TCP does nothing. (KB-FP7F, V3 live, checked 2026-10-07)
- In the v1.1 ECS create call each volume carries a metadata object with __system__encrypted set to "1" and __system__cmkid set to a KMS key id. System and data volumes are encrypted independently, and the result shows in the EVS volumes/{volume_id} metadata. (KB-FQNN, V3 live, checked 2026-06-15)
- In eu-de a batch ECS delete with delete_volume true also deleted the separately created ports attached to the servers; virtual IP ports stayed. (KB-G2PD, V3 live, checked 2026-11-19)
- The physical flavor c7t.28xlarge.4.physical (112 vCPUs, 512 GiB) provides Intel Software Guard Extensions (SGX) for secure enclaves, and the ECS user guide adds trusted boot measured from a TPM chip for C7t. The service description revision 17.08.2026 lists c7t only in EU-DE, and on 2026-09-25 the flavor list of eu-de carried it while the one of eu-nl did not. (KB-G6SH, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- Default ECS quotas from GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/limits are 100 instances, 800 vCPUs and 2,500,000 MB RAM (about 2,441 GiB); the default VPC public IP quota is 10. That is about 3 GiB per vCPU, so an estate averaging more RAM per vCPU hits the RAM quota before the vCPU quota. (KB-GBRH, V4 kept live, checked 2026-08-25)
- The ECS changeos call (POST /v2/{project_id}/cloudservers/{server_id}/changeos) accepted adminpass instead of keyname for a Windows ECS on 2026-09-25, together with mode withStopServer, and its job ended in SUCCESS. The API reference lists keyname as mandatory and no adminpass. Whether the new password took effect was not checked, because that needs a login. (KB-GL5C, V3 live, checked 2026-09-25)
- For ECS flavour availability per zone the field os_extra_specs cond:operation:az is authoritative, with values such as eu-de-01(normal),eu-de-02(normal),eu-de-03(normal). The global cond:operation:status is the fallback only for a zone that is absent from that per-AZ field. (KB-GSI6, V4 kept live, checked 2026-08-26)
- Deleting an ECS through the batch delete call with delete_volume true and delete_publicip false in eu-de removed both its system disk and its data disk created in the same create call (both then answer EVS.5404 itemNotFound) and left the elastic IP unbound but allocated. (KB-GTJD, V3 live, checked 2026-11-10)
- Microsoft Credential Guard does not run on a c9.2xlarge.2 ECS in eu-de-01 even with TPM 2.0 and Secure Boot switched on: the flavor exposes no VMX to the guest, so the Windows hypervisor that virtualization-based security needs cannot start, and Credential Guard stays off after it is enabled and the guest reboots. The TPM and Secure Boot flags of c9 and m9 serve measured boot and BitLocker, not VBS. (KB-GURS, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-10-20)
- The guest serial console is readable through the ECS API: POST /v2.1/{project_id}/servers/{server_id}/action with the os-getConsoleOutput action and a length field returns what the guest wrote to /dev/ttyS0, so a guest with no reachable network can still report. (KB-GYKQ, V3 live, checked 2026-06-11)
- A root volume created inside the ECS create call (root_volume block in POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers) carries delete_on_termination true on its attachment. Passing delete_volume false on POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/delete does not override it, and the volume is destroyed with the instance. (KB-GZ52, V3 live, checked 2026-07-29)
- In eu-de an own RSA public key can be imported as an ECS key pair through the compute API with a name and the public key, answered 200, and the key name worked as key_name in an ECS create right after. (KB-GZZT, V3 live, checked 2026-11-10)
- In an ECS flavor's extra specs cond:operation:az overrides cond:operation:status per availability zone. The format is az(xx), the value in the parentheses is the flavor status in that AZ, and empty parentheses make the entry invalid. The top-level status applies only to AZs the parameter does not list. (KB-H4FB, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-02)
- Apache Iceberg runs self-managed on a plain ECS with Spark 3.3.1, iceberg-spark-runtime-3.3_2.12 1.4.3 and hadoop-aws 3.3.2 over OBS via S3A. It passed 15 of 15 functional checks, including MERGE, row-level UPDATE and DELETE, time travel, schema and partition evolution and compaction, with Hadoop and JDBC catalogs. (KB-HAQG, V3 live, checked 2026-07-28)
- An ECS created from a shared Windows Server 2022 image on flavor s7n.large.4 was resized through the API to s9.large.2 successfully. (KB-HAVV, V3 live, checked 2026-07-21)
- A stopped s3.xlarge.2 ECS with Windows Server 2022 and an 80 GB SSD system disk in eu-de-01 reached ACTIVE 22 s after a batch os-start and SHUTOFF 64 s after a SOFT os-stop, both read by polling GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/{id} every 10 s. (KB-HDZT, V3 live, checked 2026-11-19)
- A flavor resize can fail with HTTP 400 Ecs.0319, capacity not sufficient, even when the flavor is listed with cond:operation:az=normal. An orderable flavor status does not imply capacity in the availability zone at that moment. (KB-HEJ4, V3 live, checked 2026-07-21)
- The ECS flavor listing in eu-de returned 372 flavors on 2026-09-25, 332 of them normal in at least one AZ. (KB-HIYO, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- A Windows ECS administrator password must be 8 to 26 characters long, use three character classes and must not contain the user name. (KB-HKXY, V1 docs, checked 2026-06-10)
- On 2026-11-10 in eu-de-01 a g7v.2xlarge.8 ECS from Enterprise_Windows-Server_2025_STD_amd64_uefi_prev with ESSD system and data disks reached ACTIVE within two 20-second polls of the ECS job. (KB-I64P, V3 live, checked 2026-11-10)
- On the gold image Enterprise_Windows-Server_2022_STD_amd64_uefi_latest on c9.2xlarge.2 in eu-de-01 with secure_boot_enabled and tpm_enabled true, Cloudbase-Init runs a #ps1_sysnative user_data that sets registry values, registers a startup scheduled task and reboots; the task ran after the reboot, and its Stop-Computer -Force turned the ECS to status SHUTOFF with OS-EXT-STS:power_state 4, seen 10 min 26 s after OS-SRV-USG:launched_at. A guest power-off is thus a usable result signal on a flavor without a serial log. (KB-I7KV, V3 live, checked 2026-10-20)
- A local administrator account created in Windows ECS user_data with -UserMayNotChangePassword and added to the Administrators group authenticates over RDP on T Cloud Public (TCP) without an interactive password change. (KB-IAIS, V3 live, checked 2026-08-24)
- In the eu-de ECS flavor catalogue cond:operation:status=abandon is set on nearly every flavor family, including s9, c9 and x1, while the per-AZ field cond:operation:az of the same flavors reports states such as eu-de-01(normal). (KB-IC4C, V4 kept live, checked 2026-06-11)
- A private IP address can be moved between availability zones by hand because subnets are not bound to one zone: delete the server and create it in another AZ with nics[].ip_address set to the same address, 33 seconds. The address is free only once the old server is really deleted. (KB-IJOA, V3 live, checked 2026-09-02)
- A guest on c7n.large.2 in eu-de sees the CPU brand string Intel(R) Xeon(R) Gold 6348 CPU @ 2.60GHz, family 6, model 106, stepping 6, with two threads per core. (KB-IMA2, V3 live, checked 2026-10-01)
- A public Ubuntu 24.04 image in eu-de takes its packages from debmirror.eu-de.otc-service.com. An ECS on a subnet with the internal DNS server installed qemu-system-x86 and ovmf from it without an EIP. (KB-IT7E, V3 live, checked 2026-10-01)
- First boot of a newly created Windows Server machine measured 213 s and 269 s in two runs on Windows Server 2022 and 235 s on Windows Server 2019, which was the only figure taken on 2019. (KB-IWPS, V3 live, checked 2026-09-02)
- POST /v2/{project_id}/servers/{server_id}/action with body {"os-getConsoleOutput": {"length": 200}} returns the guest console output of an ECS instance, which shows whether a user_data script ran inside the guest. (KB-J4VQ, V3 live, checked 2026-08-18)
- An ECS create with c7t.28xlarge.4.physical in eu-de-03 from Standard_Ubuntu_24.04_amd64_uefi_latest answered HTTP 200 and the job failed after 32 seconds with 0107012 Common.0021 in CreateServerWithRootVolumeAndDataVolumeTask, and no server stayed in the list. (KB-JEED, V3 live, checked 2026-11-04)
- On 2026-10-20 the flavor list of eu-de-01 marks security:tpm_supported and security:secure_boot_supported true on every c9 and m9 flavor from 8 vCPU (c9.2xlarge.2 and up, m9.2xlarge.8 and up) and false on the 2 and 4 vCPU sizes. c7n and s9 flavors carry neither key. c9.2xlarge.2 is normal in eu-de-01, eu-de-02 and eu-de-03. (KB-JQ76, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-10-20)
- In the eu-de flavour catalog s7n stops at 8 vCPU and 32 GiB while s3 reaches 32 vCPU and 256 GiB, and the only 7n size with a 1:1 vCPU to RAM ratio is s7n.small.1. Of 24 s3 sizes 18 have a 7n size with the same vCPU and RAM; the six without one are s3.large.1, s3.xlarge.1, s3.2xlarge.1, s3.4xlarge.1, s3.8xlarge.1 and s3.medium.8. (KB-K5D2, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-10-14)
- An ECS in eu-de accepts a CD-ROM through block_device_mapping_v2 with device_type=cdrom and disk_bus=ide on the native v2.1 create API. The guest gets /dev/sr0, a 1 GB ATA CD-ROM on an ata/AHCI controller (ata1), while the system disk stays virtio-blk. A blank volume backs it. (KB-K76G, V3 live, checked 2026-10-02)
- On 2026-07-19 the ECS flavor listing in eu-de reported Intel SkyLake for x1 and x1e, Cascade Lake 2.6GHz for s3, x2 and x2ne, Cascade Lake 3.0GHz for x2e, c4 and c4ne, Ice Lake 2.6GHz for s7n and c7n, and masked names for s9 and c9. (KB-KFQU, V3 live, checked 2026-07-19)
- The ECS batch delete is POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/delete with a servers list; the DELETE method on that path answers 404. After the delete, GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/{id} can still answer 200 while GET .../cloudservers/detail no longer lists the server. (KB-KVZN, V3 live, checked 2026-11-20)
- In eu-de the EVS volume type SATA (common I/O, end-of-sale) is marked sold out in eu-de-01, eu-de-02 and eu-de-03 (read 2026-09-25 and again 2026-10-12), while SAS, SSD, GPSSD, GPSSD2 and ESSD carry no sold-out mark. The mark is the key os-vendor-extended:sold_out_availability_zones inside extra_specs of GET evs /v2/{project_id}/types, one comma-separated string of zone names; a type on sale has no such key. An ECS create with root_volume.volumetype SATA in eu-de-01 answers HTTP 400 with error_code Ecs.0044 and the message that the volume type is sold out in that AZ (live check on a test tenant, 2026-10-12). (KB-KYR6, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-10-12)
- Add-WindowsCapability -Online fails on a Windows ECS that has no NAT gateway and no EIP, because Feature-on-Demand payloads, including OpenSSH Server, are fetched over the internet. (KB-KZHC, V3 live, checked 2026-08-24)
- The ECS FAQ says Windows allows at most two users in remote desktop connections, that more users need the Remote Desktop Session Host role with configured licences, that installing the role revokes the two free connections and that its grace period is 120 days. The FAQ gives no source for licences and its steps only remove the role. (KB-L5HW, V1 docs, checked 2026-11-12)
- The GPU flavor pi5e.2xlarge.4 (8 vCPU, 32 GiB, one NVIDIA L4 24 GB, Intel Ice Lake 6348) carries cond:image __support_gpu_l4=true, and on 2026-10-21 sixteen gold images in eu-de carried that property: the Ubuntu 22.04 and 24.04 GPU builds (BIOS only) and the Windows Server 2019, 2022 and 2025 gold images, UEFI builds with __support_tpm and __support_secure_boot included. The plain Standard_Ubuntu_24.04_amd64_uefi_latest does not fit this flavor. The flavor is normal in eu-de-01 and eu-de-03, absent from eu-de-02. (KB-LGNR, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-10-21)
- The flavor generations whose console picker requires the __support_kvm_hi1822_hiovs image tag are s9, c9, m9, c4ne, c6ne, x2e, x2m and x2ne. (KB-LQ6R, V3 live, checked 2026-07-20)
- In eu-de the ECS instance listing is GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/detail and returns the instances under the response key servers. Each entry carries name, id, status, flavor with vcpus and ram, addresses, metadata.vpc_id and os-extended-volumes:volumes_attached. (KB-LSFW, V3 live, checked 2026-09-25)
- An s3.large.1 ECS with two NICs in two subnets, booted in eu-de-01 from a private image imported with is_config false and os_version Other Linux(64 bit), reached job SUCCESS and status ACTIVE about 31 seconds after POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers, and its os-getConsoleOutput stayed empty for 4 minutes after boot. (KB-LYXX, V3 live, checked 2026-11-10)
- An in-place ECS resize from s3.large.2 to s3.medium.2 (stop, resize call, start) keeps the server id, the private IPs and port_ids of both NICs, the attached security group, the allowed_address_pairs source and destination check override on both ports, and the EIP binding. Nothing has to be re-applied. (KB-M2RZ, V3 live, checked 2026-07-29)
- A g7v.2xlarge.8 ECS in eu-de-01 from the public image Enterprise_Windows-Server_2022_STD_amd64_uefi_prev with ESSD 100 GB system and 250 GB data disk, without EIP and without user data, was ACTIVE 31 seconds after the create call, and its server_tags reached both EVS disks. The flavor read cond:operation:status abandon but cond:operation:az eu-de-01(normal), and the create succeeded. (KB-M3EC, V3 live, checked 2026-11-18)
- An ECS bound to an IAM agency gets temporary AK/SK from the link-local metadata service at GET /openstack/latest/securitykey. This is the TCP workload-identity path, the analog of an instance profile with temporary credentials, and Terraform supports it. (KB-M7O3, V1 docs, checked 2026-07-24)
- A public Ubuntu 24.04 image ran its OTC BOOTCMD step until 637 seconds after boot on a subnet without DNS servers. After the subnet got the internal DNS server through dnsList, the user data script of the next guest from the same base ran 84 seconds after boot. (KB-MIZ3, V3 live, checked 2026-10-01)
- ECS key pairs belong to the IAM user that imports them: a key pair imported with one user of a tenant does not show in the key pair list of another user of the same tenant, so a server created by a key-service user can only take that user's key pairs. (KB-MKCN, V3 live, checked 2026-11-10)
- On a freshly created Windows ECS in T Cloud Public (TCP) eu-de the built-in Administrator account comes up flagged password must change, even when adminPass is set in the create call. FreeRDP returns ERRCONNECT_PASSWORD_MUST_CHANGE and WinRM over NTLM returns InvalidCredentials until the password is changed interactively. (KB-MLBL, V3 live, checked 2026-08-24)
- ECS flavor availability per AZ can change within the same day. s9.medium.1 booted in eu-de-01 and was sold out about 30 minutes later, which os_extra_specs cond:operation:az reflected; s7n.small.1 was orderable at that moment. (KB-MQ2H, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-07-17)
- The eu-de service description lists the s7n general-purpose ECS family up to size 2xlarge, and lists the s9, s3 and c9 families at 8xlarge.2 with 32 vCPU and 64 GiB. (KB-MQND, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-09-15)
- POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers accepts security_options with both secure_boot_enabled true and tpm_enabled true for c9.2xlarge.2 with the gold image Enterprise_Windows-Server_2022_STD_amd64_uefi_latest in eu-de-01, and the server detail returns security_options with both flags true. The ECS create API reference documents only tpm_enabled under security_options; the secure_boot_enabled field is undocumented but works. (KB-MYCE, V3 live, checked 2026-10-20)
- The os-getConsoleOutput action returns HTTP 200 with an empty output string for a guest that writes nothing to ttyS0, because only what the guest sends to the serial port is kept. The same call returns the written lines when user_data writes to /dev/ttyS0. (KB-NDWB, V3 live, checked 2026-07-02)
- s9 flavors cap at 4 GB RAM per vCPU, so a memory-optimized m4 instance at 8 GB per vCPU matches m9 and would lose half its RAM on a move to s9. (KB-NH6R, V1 docs, checked 2026-07-21)
- Elastic Cloud Server (ECS) provides virtual and physical compute instances (virtual servers) made of vCPUs, memory, block storage on Elastic Volume Service and a public or private operating system image, chosen from preconfigured flavors. The flavor groups are general purpose, dedicated general purpose, memory optimized, large memory, storage optimized, flexible purpose and GPU accelerated (NVIDIA T4, L4, A40, V100, A100 and H100 cards), plus physical instances; public images include Microsoft Windows Server and the Linux distributions SLES, Oracle Linux and Red Hat Enterprise Linux. The Regions and Endpoints page lists API endpoints for ECS in eu-de and eu-nl. (KB-NHAY, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-10-14)
- An existing ECS resizes to a newer-generation flavor in place; the create and resize APIs ignore the flavor cond:image requirement __support_kvm_hi1822_hiovs, so an untagged image resizes fine. The tag only makes the console flavor picker list s9, and for a Windows image the console greys s9 out even with the tag. (KB-NRJ5, V3 live, checked 2026-07-21)
- A Dedicated Host of type s2-medium in eu-de-01 (POST /v1.0/{project_id}/dedicated-hosts, auto_placement off, quantity 1) was available at once with 72 vCPUs, 335872 MB memory, 2 sockets and 12 cores and offered s2 flavors from s2.medium.1 to s2.8xlarge.8; an ECS created with os:scheduler_hints tenancy dedicated and dedicated_host_id was placed on it (GET .../dedicated-hosts/{id}/servers listed it), and DELETE released the host after the ECS was gone. (KB-NSBC, V3 live, checked 2026-11-21)
- Creating a Windows ECS with adminPass in the create body works, but the injected password is flagged "user must change password at first logon", so the first RDP login forces a password change. (KB-NTZM, V3 live, checked 2026-06-10)
- Text pasted into the TCP console Remote Login (noVNC) is typed as US-layout key presses, so on a Windows guest with a German layout '-', 'y', 'z', quotes, '=' and '|' come out wrong; commands made only of other letters, digits, dots and spaces (ipconfig, sc.exe qc NAME, diskmgmt.msc, devmgmt.msc, msinfo32) arrive intact. (KB-NVDN, V3 live, checked 2026-11-13)
- The platform accepts two ECS instances with the same name in one project, so an ECS name can match more than one instance. (KB-NYRP, V3 live, checked 2026-09-02)
- An ECS create that names a key pair made in another tenant fails with HTTP 400 Ecs.0314 'the keypair ... does not match the user_id': key pairs belong to the tenant's user, so each tenant needs its own key pair from the same public key. (KB-NZ4W, V3 live, checked 2026-11-12)
- An ECS create in eu-de through the native v2.1 API with block_device_mapping_v2 disk_bus sata on the boot volume (source image, destination volume) reaches ACTIVE, but TCP ignores the bus: the system disk is /dev/vda on virtio-blk at PCI 0000:02:01.0 and OVMF boots it as UEFI Misc Device. (KB-O442, V3 live, checked 2026-10-07)
- A Windows ECS created with key_name receives a Cloudbase-Init generated random password that carries no must-change flag. It is fetched with the os-server-password API and decrypted locally with the private key, for example with openssl pkeyutl -decrypt. (KB-OGU3, V3 live, checked 2026-06-11)
- The documentation describes the general-purpose ECS category on T Cloud Public (TCP) as a baseline-performance line with bursting, and the C9 family as the dedicated line for high load. (KB-ONCW, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-15)
- POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/delete with delete_volume true also removes attached data disks whose delete_on_termination is false: an ECS with one system disk and two such data disks and all three disks were gone from the list calls within about 64 seconds. (KB-OPQC, V3 live, checked 2026-11-05)
- ECS compute is billed per second, so a stopped instance stops accruing compute charges. EVS volumes attached to it keep billing while it is stopped, so stopping an instance reduces the compute part of its cost only. (KB-OTPK, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-06-29)
- An ECS create on c9.2xlarge.2 in eu-de, a flavor with TPM and Secure Boot support, from an image with hw_disk_bus sata fails like the smaller flavors: the job ends in FAIL after about 30 s with Common.0021 and 'server is error, code is 0300000', and TCP rolls the server back to DELETED. (KB-P2OZ, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-10-22)
- On an s7n.large.2 Ubuntu 24.04 ECS a user_data script that opened /dev/ttyS0 during cloud-init failed with OSError Errno 5 (Input/output error); kernel messages written to /dev/kmsg with level <6> did not show in os-getConsoleOutput either; writing whole lines to /dev/console (and to /dev/kmsg with level <1>) did show. Each write must be a whole line: print() through a custom writer sends fragments. (KB-P33N, V3 live, checked 2026-11-13)
- A socat proxy relaying migration traffic on an ECS failed under load with fork(): Resource temporarily unavailable, from file-descriptor and process limit exhaustion. The 'Connection reset by peer' errors seen by clients were a symptom of that exhaustion, not the cause. It was run as a systemd unit with LimitNOFILE=65536 and LimitNPROC=65536. (KB-P56B, V3 live, checked 2026-02-04)
- On 2026-10-04 no H100 flavor could be ordered in eu-de: every p5e (H100 NVL) and p5s (H100 PCIe) size read sellout in eu-de-01, its only zone, including p5s.20xlarge.12, which read normal on 2026-09-25. (KB-PA4Y, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-10-04)
- The resource opentelekomcloud_ecs_instance_v1 reports the private address as nics[0].ip_address. A reference to a wrong attribute behind a splat passes terraform validate and is rejected at plan time. (KB-PDZX, V3 live, checked 2026-09-16)
- Secure Boot is available for an ECS and is switched off by default. (KB-PJIW, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-21)
- An ECS root image is swapped with POST /v2/{project_id}/cloudservers/{server_id}/changeos on ecs.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com, body key os-change with imageid, and the response carries job_id. The API reference lists keyname, userid, imageid and metadata in os-change but no adminpass, and the v1 form of the same path returns HTTP 404 with error code APIGW.0101. (KB-PJK5, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- An ECS of flavor s3.medium.1 with a SAS system disk boots the public image Standard_Ubuntu_22.04_latest in eu-de-01 and answers on TCP port 22 inside its VPC when a security group rule lets port 22 in from the subnet; no EIP is needed. After POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/delete the server read still answered 200 with status DELETED about 5 minutes later, while the server had left cloudservers/detail and its system disk was gone. (KB-PLHK, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-10-12)
- The changeos call expects the server in state SHUTOFF; a request against a running server carries mode withStopServer instead. (KB-PR7C, V3 live, checked 2026-09-25)
- The image property hw_disk_bus ide puts the system disk of an ECS on c7n.large.2 in eu-de on the primary IDE channel of the PIIX3 controller, ata1 at port 0x1f0. A UEFI Ubuntu 24.04 guest booted from it as /dev/sda. (KB-PTV4, V3 live, checked 2026-10-01)
- An ECS create in eu-de from an image with hw_machine_type q35 fails: on c7n.large.2 and c9.large.2 the job ends in Ecs.0204 with 'server is error, code is 0107012', on s9.large.2 it times out after 31 minutes, and TCP rolls the server back. (KB-PW4E, V3 live, checked 2026-10-01)
- An ECS flavor change is POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/{server_id}/resize with resize.flavorRef and returns a job_id. The server has to be in SHUTOFF, stopped with POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/action and os-stop. The flow is stop, resize, start, with the ECS jobs endpoint polled in between. (KB-PW6W, V3 live, checked 2026-07-20)
- An ECS created with server_tags in the body of POST /v1.1/{project_id}/cloudservers carries exactly those tags and no others; created through a signed call in write mode on a customer tenant, no extra tag was added. (KB-PWJ4, V3 live, checked 2026-11-13)
- In eu-de-01 one SOFT os-stop through POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/action for four small ECS at once (c4.large.2 with an OPNsense image, s3.medium.2 Debian, s3.large.4 Windows Server, s9.medium.1 Ubuntu) returned one job_id, and all four read SHUTOFF within 48 s when polled every 15 s; the Linux servers took 16 s, the OPNsense one 32 s and the Windows one 48 s. (KB-QA64, V3 live, checked 2026-11-19)
- U-Boot 2019.04 already contains a virtio block driver: the build options VIRTIO_PCI and VIRTIO_BLK and the command 'virtio scan' (CMD_VIRTIO, on by default when VIRTIO is set). A loader on that release can find a virtio-blk disk once these options are enabled. (KB-QAFV, V1 docs, checked 2026-10-07)
- POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/delete with delete_volume true deletes every volume attached to the server, data volumes of another server attached for a helper job included; detach such a volume first. A volume detached and attached again between helpers and back as /dev/vda keeps its data. (KB-QG3K, V3 live, checked 2026-11-13)
- An empty subnet dnsList stalls cloud-init: the same image and flavor booted in about 700 seconds without resolvers and about 103 seconds with them. Check subnet DNS before blaming the flavor for slow ECS boots. (KB-QHR6, V3 live, checked 2026-07-17)
- The NIC limit of a flavor is published as os_extra_specs quota:vif_max_num in GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/flavors, for example 2 for s3.large.2 with 2 vCPU and 4 GB. The same limit is mirrored in the create response as attachableQuantity.free_nic. (KB-R4JC, V4 kept live, checked 2026-07-20)
- The flavor API of eu-de marks security:tpm_supported true on c9 and m9 from 8 vCPU, but the TPM page of the ECS docs lists Pi5e as the only instance type that supports TPM. The Secure Boot page lists Pi5e, C9 and M9 (8 vCPU or more in eu-de-01). TPM is switched on per ECS at create (Trusted Settings), not by the flavor alone. (KB-RBLP, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-10-20)
- On 2026-10-04 in eu-de the one-GPU A100 flavor p3.2xlarge.8 (1 x A100 80GB PCIe) read normal in eu-de-03, its only zone, confirmed through both the ECS flavor list and the Nova os-extra_specs call, while p3.4xlarge.8, p3.8xlarge.8 and p3.16xlarge.8 read sellout. (KB-RFLA, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-10-04)
- root_volume.size in the ECS create body and bandwidth.size in the EIP assign body are typed Integer. A quoted value such as "40" is rejected with a type error that names the field. (KB-RIBI, V3 live, checked 2026-08-18)
- A job_id is polled at the service that issued it, each under the same /v1/{project_id}/jobs/{job_id} shape: ECS actions such as changeos, volume attach and power operations on ecs.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com, EVS volume create and delete on evs.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com, and IMS image actions on ims.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com. (KB-RIC7, V3 live, checked 2026-07-30)
- POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers wants in nics.subnet_id the VPC subnet id, which is the network_id of a port; the subnet_id in the fixed_ips of a port is the Neutron IPv4 subnet id, and the create refuses it with HTTP 400 Ecs.1200 (detail Ecs.0038) "subnet ... is not exist". GET /v1/{project_id}/subnets maps the two as id and neutron_subnet_id. (KB-RIDB, V3 live, checked 2026-11-18)
- The documented ways to reset a Windows administrator password on an ECS are changing it inside the running OS, or detaching the system disk and attaching it to a temporary ECS. (KB-RIVY, V1 docs, checked 2026-06-10)
- Each ECS flavor publishes a cond:image condition in os_extra_specs, and all s9 sizes list __support_kvm=true and __support_kvm_hi1822_hiovs=true. The condition refers to the image's KVM tags and virtual_env_type, not to whether the image is public or private. (KB-RK7V, V4 kept live, checked 2026-07-20)
- On 2026-10-27 the read key of a TCP test tenant got HTTP 403 Ecs.0110 'do not have the required roles, forbidden to perform this action' on GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/{server_id}, while the same call with the lab key worked and the delete job could be read with the read key. A read key can lose ECS read roles while other ECS GET calls still pass. (KB-ROCD, V3 live, checked 2026-10-27)
- An ECS create with a dot in the server name (for example a flavor name as suffix) answers HTTP 400 Ecs.0005 with the detail Ecs.0915: the name of the server includes an invalid character. (KB-RSO5, V3 live, checked 2026-11-21)
- Polling a job_id at another service's jobs endpoint returns a body holding the job_id with status null, and keeps returning that on repeat polls instead of an error. A status appears only when the jobs endpoint belongs to the API that returned the job_id. (KB-RVGO, V3 live, checked 2026-07-30)
- In eu-de on 2026-10-14 the ECS flavors m9.32xlarge.8 (128 vCPU, 1 TB RAM) and m9.48xlarge.8 show status sellout in all three availability zones, while m9.16xlarge.8 shows normal in all three. The m9.3xlarge, m9.6xlarge, m9.12xlarge and m9.24xlarge sizes appear only in eu-de-03, with status abandon. On 2026-09-11 m9.32xlarge.8 was sellout in eu-de-01 only. (KB-S22V, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-10-14)
- Creating an ECS from an image that does not fit the flavor fails with Ecs.0005, flavor does not match image. Images named *_GPU_* fit GPU flavors only, and images with virtual_env_type Ironic fit physical flavors only. (KB-S3RC, V3 live, checked 2026-06-11)
- The order of the nics array in the ECS create request determines the interface order inside the guest: the first entry becomes port1, the second port2. This decides the role of port1 for appliances that expect a fixed interface convention. (KB-SCHO, V3 live, checked 2026-07-23)
- A job id returned by an EVS volume create or delete is polled on the EVS service endpoint at /v1/{project_id}/jobs/{job_id}. A job id returned by an ECS operation such as changeos, volume attach or start and stop is polled on the ECS service endpoint at the same path. (KB-SECN, V3 live, checked 2026-09-25)
- An ECS in eu-de cannot be created with config_drive true: the native create ends in ERROR after 12 scheduling attempts with the fault 'Nova supports 24 virtio-blk disks', and the docs mark config_drive as not supported. (KB-SGDX, V3 live, checked 2026-10-01)
- An ECS can be deleted while its elastic IP is kept, by setting delete_publicip to false in the delete request. (KB-SGOE, V3 live, checked 2026-06-11)
- In eu-de the ECS resize path under v1.1 answers 404 APIGW.0101 (API does not exist); the documented v1 path for resizing a cloudserver is the one that takes the request. (KB-SIIO, V3 live, checked 2026-11-10)
- An ECS supports at most 12 NICs in total, and typical flavors cap at 2 to 4, which limits one-IP-per-pod designs to low pod density. (KB-SIZU, V1 docs, checked 2026-06-12)
- In eu-de-01, 326 of 329 ECS flavors carry cond:operation:status = abandon while instances still run on them, and only 3 carry normal. The per-AZ condition field, not the global status field, reflects whether a flavor can be used. (KB-SPTH, V4 kept live, checked 2026-08-18)
- On T Cloud Public (TCP) in eu-de, at equal vCPU and memory, the s9 general-purpose ECS family has a lower pay-as-you-go hourly rate than the s7n and s3 general-purpose families and than the dedicated c9 family. (KB-SRKV, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-09-15)
- The ECS changeos operation is served at /v2/{project_id}/cloudservers/{server_id}/changeos in TCP eu-de. The same path under v1 returns HTTP 404. (KB-SW4Q, V3 live, checked 2026-09-25)
- On a Windows guest, Cloudbase-Init sets the guest name during first boot and reboots the machine, so a deployment that sets the guest name carries one extra reboot. (KB-SXN2, V3 live, checked 2026-09-02)
- The service description names a 99.95% availability figure for ECS, OBS and EVS, and states that in all other respects there is no minimum availability. A component counts as available (excused event) while it is reachable in at least one availability zone. (KB-T4MF, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-09-02)
- On c9.2xlarge.2 in eu-de the EVS disks of an ECS are not emulated by QEMU: they are vfio-pci passthrough virtual functions of the SDI card that the guest sees as virtio-blk. An image with hw_disk_bus ide is ignored there: the block_device API reports /dev/hda, but OVMF finds the system disk as virtio-blk behind a PCI bridge, and the PIIX3 IDE controller of the i440FX machine stays empty. (KB-T4ZK, V3 live, checked 2026-10-21)
- On 2026-07-20 every s9 size returned Ecs.0319 in eu-de-01, while the same create request succeeded in eu-de-03. On 2026-10-14 an ECS create of s9.medium.1 in eu-de-01 succeeded and the server was ACTIVE 38 seconds after the call, while every s9 size listed eu-de-01 as normal in cond:operation:az. A stock-out of a flavor in one AZ is a state of that day, not a lasting property of the flavor. (KB-T6NU, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-10-14)
- In eu-de DELETE /v1/{project_id}/publicips/{id} of an unbound EIP with a dedicated traffic bandwidth answers 204 and the EIP answers GET 404 at once; DELETE /v2/images/{id} of a private system disk image answers 204 and GET 404 within a few seconds. (KB-TGHT, V3 live, checked 2026-11-19)
- An ECS of flavor x1.large.4 (4 vCPU, 8 GB) in eu-de sustained 100 to 110 MB/s for a full 400 GB anonymous download from a public HuggingFace repository, using hf download with HF_HUB_ENABLE_HF_TRANSFER=1 and 4 workers. (KB-TGQ5, V3 live, checked 2026-05-01)
- In eu-de the batch delete job of a stopped g7v.2xlarge.8 Windows ECS with two ESSD disks and delete_volume true ended SUCCESS after about 40 seconds; both disks answered GET 404 at once, while GET of the server still answered 200 with status DELETED. (KB-THOY, V3 live, checked 2026-11-19)
- The ECS instance UUID in the guest metadata (/openstack/latest/meta_data.json, field uuid) matches attachments[].server_id in the EVS cloudvolumes/detail response, which confirms which volume is attached to that VM. (KB-TJPS, V3 live, checked 2026-06-08)
- The eu-de public image Standard_Ubuntu_24.04_amd64_bios_prev names the first NIC enp0s3, not eth0, and a server created from it without a key pair prints a generated initial password of user ubuntu on the serial console, so console output read through os-getConsoleOutput must be treated as a secret. (KB-TUVA, V3 live, checked 2026-11-22)
- Teardown of a baseline deployment follows dependency order: ECS, elastic IP, key pair, subnet, security group, VPC. (KB-U3Z7, V3 live, checked 2026-08-18)
- First boot of a new Windows Server 2022 ECS on s9.large.2 reached RDP after 213 s, stopping it to SHUTOFF took 21 s, and starting the prepared stopped machine reached RDP after 23 s. First boot carries Cloudbase-Init hostname setting and a reboot. (KB-U7NB, V3 live, checked 2026-09-02)
- A resize request from an s9 flavor back to s3 or s7n returns error Ecs.0804. Only s3 to s9 and s7n to s9 were observed to succeed, and cross-family or older-generation targets can be refused the same way. (KB-UCGS, V3 live, checked 2026-07-21)
- Because cond:operation:az wins per AZ, an ECS flavor whose top-level cond:operation:status reads abandon can still be running instances in an AZ that the az parameter lists as available. (KB-UE4H, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-02)
- After a delete, GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/{server_id} answered HTTP 200 with status DELETED for at least seven minutes on 2026-09-25 instead of HTTP 404, so a wait that looks for 404 alone does not see the delete. (KB-UFOT, V3 live, checked 2026-09-25)
- Cloudbase-Init executes #ps1_sysnative user_data on T Cloud Public (TCP) Windows public images. Local users created by such a script authenticated afterwards over RDP. (KB-UKPX, V3 live, checked 2026-08-24)
- The adminPass field of the ECS create API only satisfies platform validation. An appliance image that ignores cloud-init password injection keeps its own factory default credentials at first login. (KB-UQXM, V3 live, checked 2026-07-23)
- TCP exposes TPM 2.0 and Secure Boot with the UEFI Windows images on c9 and m9 flavors from 8 vCPU and on the pi5e GPU flavor family. (KB-USAA, V4 kept live, checked 2026-09-25)
- os-getConsoleOutput returns no lines for the Meraki vMX 26.1.4 UEFI image on c9.2xlarge.2 in eu-de, a flavor with TPM and Secure Boot support, as on c9.large.2: the c9 family gives no serial log for this image at all sizes tried, while c7n.large.2 does. (KB-UWU2, V3 live, checked 2026-10-20)
- A Hyper-V Generation 1 Windows Server 2019 VM with the VirtIO drivers installed on the source boots on TCP from a BIOS image on s7n.large.4 in about two minutes: its IDE and SCSI disks come up as VirtIO SCSI disks, MBR data disks keep their drive letters and file hashes, no device reports a problem and the new NIC takes DHCP although the source had a static address. (KB-V3WU, V3 live, checked 2026-11-11)
- In eu-de on 2026-09-25 the ECS s9 family comes in 1, 2 and 4 GiB of RAM per vCPU (suffixes .1, .2 and .4, 1 to 64 vCPUs). The m9 family gives 8 GiB per vCPU, except m9.48xlarge.8 with 192 vCPUs and 1,504 GiB. (KB-V64D, V3 live, checked 2026-09-25)
- A cold rebuild of a Linux server took 111 seconds: image 12 seconds, ECS creation 33 seconds, guest boot 66 seconds. The run used a backup taken from a stopped server. (KB-V6WG, V3 live, checked 2026-09-02)
- A flavor that the API lists as orderable in an availability zone is a catalog entry rather than a stock guarantee. An ECS create against such a flavor can still fail with error code Ecs.0319. (KB-V7XS, V3 live, checked 2026-08-18)
- Savings plans (SP) is a beta in the preview and beta section of the T Cloud Public (TCP) service description that gives discounted pricing for eligible services in exchange for a usage commitment over a defined term; unlike reserved instances, the discount is not tied to one instance flavor. The revision of 09.10.2026 names the ECS families c4, m4, c9 and m9 as eligible, announces it as expected to be available from 1 December 2026, grants beta participation at Telekom's discretion on a Service Desk request and leaves reserved packages unaffected. (KB-VBBT, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-10-14)
- On a Windows ECS, Cloudbase-Init takes the guest hostname from the ECS name. A restore under a different ECS name renamed the guest and added a second boot, with RDP answering after 191 s against 149 s when the ECS name matched the original. (KB-VBVZ, V3 live, checked 2026-09-02)
- The x1, x1e and x1m ECS flavor families are priced pay-as-you-go only in section 6 of the service description and carry no reserved price rows. For a given vCPU and RAM target the cheapest pay-as-you-go flavor and the cheapest reservable one are therefore usually different flavors. (KB-VYRW, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-10-14)
- An x1 ECS in eu-de got no ARP reply from c9 and s7n ECS placed in the same subnet, security group (self-rule present) and AZ, and ping reported Destination Host Unreachable, although those guests booted and reached the metadata service. (KB-W3LX, V3 live, checked 2026-06-11)
- An ECS create call takes imageRef as an image UUID, so an image name is resolved through IMS first. Public image names encode architecture and firmware, so Standard_Ubuntu_22.04_latest and Standard_Ubuntu_24.04_amd64_uefi_latest address different images. (KB-W7Q3, V3 live, checked 2026-08-18)
- POST /v1.1/{project_id}/cloudservers with "dry_run": true next to "server" answers 202 with an empty body and creates no server (a name search 30 s later finds none); a wrong parameter in the same body, for example an unknown flavorRef, answers 400 (Ecs.0303), so the dry run checks the request without creating anything. (KB-WDG6, V3 live, checked 2026-11-13)
- ECS create is asynchronous and returns a job_id instead of a finished server. The job is polled for its status, and a FAIL result carries error_code and fail_reason. (KB-WE2L, V3 live, checked 2026-08-18)
- The ECS flavor catalog in eu-de holds no ARM64 compute. A live listing on 2026-10-14 returned 372 flavors with no aarch64, arm64 or kunpeng string anywhere in the payload. (KB-WKYI, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-10-14)
- ECS limits are read with GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/limits on ecs.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com. The 200 response carries an absolute object with totalInstancesUsed, maxTotalInstances, totalCoresUsed, maxTotalCores, totalRAMUsed and maxTotalRAMSize. (KB-WPJH, V3 live, checked 2026-08-18)
- When the root volume attachment of an ECS carries delete_on_termination true, the root disk is deleted together with the server even though the delete call passes delete_volume false. (KB-WSAJ, V3 live, checked 2026-08-18)
- An ECS on c7n.large.2 in eu-de serves the same user data at /openstack/latest/user_data and /latest/user-data and shows no CD-ROM device and no config drive. (KB-WT3L, V3 live, checked 2026-10-01)
- On c9.2xlarge.2 in eu-de a block_device_mapping_v2 entry with device_type cdrom and disk_bus ide does not give a CD-ROM: the 1 GB volume reaches the guest as a second virtio-blk disk and OVMF finds no ATAPI device. On c7n.large.2 the same entry gave /dev/sr0 (KB-K76G). (KB-WTXB, V3 live, checked 2026-10-21)
- The serial console output of an ECS (os-getConsoleOutput) returns only a tail of about 200 lines, and Cloudbase-Init writes to the port during boot, so a report a guest script writes early can be lost; a guest report should repeat its block every few minutes. (KB-WU7W, V3 live, checked 2026-11-21)
- A tenant can sit far below the eu-de ECS defaults: GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/limits answered 10 instances, 40 vCPUs and 163840 MB RAM on one customer tenant, so a quota plan must read the live values of the tenant. (KB-WV5P, V3 live, checked 2026-11-04)
- Among ECS flavours only s9, c9 and m9 carry info:features = sdi together with hinic driver mode. The NIC tag they share with x2e, x2ne and c4ne and the quota:spdk_queues property present on s7n, c7n and m7n do not separate them, so the link to the SDRS restriction is correlation. (KB-X2FM, V3 live, checked 2026-08-26)
- A resize keeps an instance in its existing availability zone, so the target flavor must have capacity in that AZ. (KB-XLKX, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- os-getConsoleOutput returns an empty output for an ECS on c9.large.2 in eu-de that boots the Meraki vMX 26.1.4 UEFI image, while the same image on c7n.large.2 returns its OVMF and U-Boot lines. Use c7n.large.2 when the serial log of this image is needed. (KB-XPGL, V3 live, checked 2026-10-07)
- Rebuilding a Windows Server 2022 machine and renaming the guest added about 42 s: 149 s to RDP without the rename, 191 s with it. (KB-Y3BF, V3 live, checked 2026-09-02)
- The guest console output of an ECS is read with POST /v2/{project_id}/servers/{id}/action and the body {"os-getConsoleOutput": {"length": 200}}, which returns the cloud-init lines of the boot. (KB-YHCE, V3 live, checked 2026-08-18)
- A download from a public model hub into an ECS in eu-de held about 100 MB/s sustained over roughly 400 GB, with no account or token used on the remote side. (KB-YJF3, V3 live, checked 2026-05-01)
- The U-Boot of the Meraki vMX 26.1.4 KVM image (2019.04-g8ff3e49edae) has no virtio command: 'virtio scan' answers Unknown command. Its bootcmd loads the system only from SCSI/AHCI: scsi scan, then ext4load from scsi 0:2 of the FIT image in the new, safe and old system folders, and bootm with the config x86_vm_config. (KB-YJUR, V3 live, checked 2026-10-07)
- The s9 general-purpose family caps at 4 GB RAM per vCPU, so a memory-optimized m4 flavor at 8 GB per vCPU matches m9, not s9: m4.large.8 with 2 vCPU and 16 GB maps to m9.large.8, while s9.large.4 would halve the memory. (KB-YKIN, V3 live, checked 2026-07-21)
- The Meraki vMX 26.1.4 KVM image (UEFI) on c7n.large.2 in eu-de starts its U-Boot 2019.04 EFI payload and stops at 'scanning bus for devices...', with the system disk on IDE and on virtio-blk alike. (KB-YQOS, V3 live, checked 2026-10-01)
- In eu-de a g7v.2xlarge.8 ECS can be resized only to g7v sizes, so a g7v machine cannot be moved to a flavor without vGPU. (KB-Z4CF, V3 live, checked 2026-11-10)
- TCP in eu-de refuses a SATA system disk but attaches a CD-ROM on an ATA/AHCI controller through block_device_mapping_v2: the two paths to an AHCI device behave differently. (KB-Z72S, V3 live, checked 2026-10-02)
- In eu-de on 2026-09-25, each entry of GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/detail carried flavor with name, vcpus and ram, addresses with OS-EXT-IPS:type and OS-EXT-IPS:port_id, security_groups with id and name, OS-EXT-AZ:availability_zone and status with OS-EXT-STS:power_state, ACTIVE with 1 and SHUTOFF with 4. Its os-extended-volumes:volumes_attached entries hold only id, device, bootIndex and delete_on_termination, so the disk size needs GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/{server_id}/block_device or the EVS API while the disk type and encryption need the EVS API. (KB-Z73M, V3 live, checked 2026-09-25)
- For an ECS re-imaged to Windows with changeos, the console Get Password function is not available, because the instance was not originally provisioned from a Windows image and holds no key-encrypted password metadata. (KB-ZCKR, V3 live, checked 2026-02-04)
- The Nova-compatible v2.1 surface of the ECS endpoint in eu-de accepts POST /v2.1/{project_id}/servers/{server_id}/action with the os-getConsoleOutput body and an optional length. The response returns the last lines of the instance serial port ttyS0 in the output key. (KB-ZCYK, V3 live, checked 2026-06-11)
- An ECS is resized with POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/{server_id}/resize and body {"resize": {"flavorRef": ...}} after a stop; s3.medium.1 to s3.large.2 finished as a job in about 67 seconds. The path /v1.1/{project_id}/cloudservers/{server_id}/resize answers 404 APIGW.0101. (KB-ZQAN, V3 live, checked 2026-11-20)
- In eu-de a job id polled on the endpoint of the other service returns HTTP 400 with error code Common.0011 (ECS: No Job found by jobId, EVS: query job fail) instead of a JSON body with status null (2026-09-25). (KB-ZXBG, V3 live, checked 2026-09-25)
- os_extra_specs also reports hw:cpu_cores, hw:cpu_threads and hw:cpu_sockets as physical cores, threads per core and sockets. An 8 vCPU flavor of the *.2xlarge.2 size resolves to 4 cores by 2 threads on 1 socket, one NUMA node. (KB-ZYK5, V3 live, checked 2026-07-19)
- cloud-init skips a user_data script whose #! line is indented. The guest log then shows cc_scripts_user.py[WARNING]: Failed to run module scripts-user and a failed cloud-final.service, while the ECS still reaches status ACTIVE with its security group and EIP bound. (KB-ZYW7, V3 live, checked 2026-08-18)

## ims (172)

- A private image is removed with DELETE /v2/images/{image_id} on the Glance-compatible IMS surface in eu-de, which answers HTTP 204. (KB-23TF, V3 live, checked 2026-06-12)
- On 2026-11-20 all 144 public images listed by GET /v2/cloudimages?__imagetype=gold in eu-de had virtual_env_type FusionCompute (141) or Ironic (3), and none carried a __support_xen tag; 97 carried __support_kvm true. (KB-26KI, V3 live, checked 2026-11-20)
- Trap: an ECS made from a Windows Server 2008 R2 image without VirtIO drivers that starts once and is stopped hard during that failed boot does not boot after the drivers are injected offline: no event log entry, no dump, no shutdown of a startup script within 20 minutes (the boot manager takes its error recovery path, whose environment has no VirtIO driver). Setting the Windows loader object in Boot\BCD offline to recoveryenabled No (element 16000009 = 00) and bootstatuspolicy IgnoreAllFailures (element 250000e0 = 1) fixed it: the next start booted and ran the startup script after about 5 minutes. (KB-27BI, V3 live, checked 2026-11-18)
- A system disk image of a Sysprepped g7v.2xlarge.8 Windows Server 2022 ECS (60 GB SSD) was created with POST /v2/cloudimages/action in 362 s; the private image showed __support_kvm true, hw_firmware_type uefi and no __support_kvm_gpu_type, and a g7v.2xlarge.8 ECS was created from it in 32 s. (KB-27SD, V3 live, checked 2026-11-12)
- In eu-de the public Windows Server 2019 Standard images are named Enterprise_Windows-Server_2019_STD_amd64_bios_latest, _bios_prev, _uefi_latest and _uefi_prev (plus STD-CORE variants), each with min_disk 40 and hw_firmware_type bios or uefi as the name says; GET /v2/cloudimages?__imagetype=gold&__os_type=Windows&status=active lists them. (KB-2C7T, V3 live, checked 2026-11-13)
- On a Windows Server 2008 R2 SP1 ECS on TCP whose disk drivers were injected offline, a manual install in the VNC console works for the VirtIO NIC: Device Manager, Ethernet Controller, Update Driver Software, Browse my computer, the folder with the virtio-win 0.1.215 2k8R2 NetKVM package; Windows reports Red Hat VirtIO Ethernet Adapter installed and the adapter takes its DHCP address with the subnet gateway at once, without a restart. The same package is refused by the hardware-initiated install without a user. (KB-2CMC, V3 live, checked 2026-11-18)
- Trap: an ECS made from a Windows Server 2008 R2 image without VirtIO drivers that starts once and is stopped hard during that failed boot does not boot after the drivers are injected offline: no event log entry, no dump, no shutdown of a startup script within 20 minutes (the boot manager takes its error recovery path, whose environment has no VirtIO driver). Setting the Windows loader object in Boot\BCD offline to recoveryenabled No (element 16000009 = 00) and bootstatuspolicy IgnoreAllFailures (element 250000e0 = 1) fixed it: the next start booted and ran the startup script after about 5 minutes. (KB-2G27, V3 live, checked 2026-11-18)
- Private images of a tenant are listed with GET /v2/cloudimages?__imagetype=private on the IMS endpoint in eu-de, optionally narrowed with a name parameter. The image type parameter carries a double underscore prefix. (KB-2L2B, V4 kept live, checked 2026-09-25)
- A Windows service created offline with ImagePath cmd.exe /c script (no service wrapper) is killed by the Service Control Manager about 30 s after start on Windows Server 2019; a pnputil /add-driver /install inside it was cut off at device Start and left the NIC half installed. A launcher that hands the work to start /b cmd.exe runs to the end. pnputil /scan-devices does not exist on build 17763. (KB-2UUV, V3 live, checked 2026-11-16)
- Windows Server 2008 R2 SP1 applied from install.wim with the 2k8R2 VirtIO drivers added offline by DISM gets viostor and vioscsi as boot start (Start 0) with critical device entries, but after the first boot on Hyper-V (no VirtIO device) both services are back at Start 3; they must be set to 0 again before the disk leaves Hyper-V. (KB-35PF, V3 live, checked 2026-11-16)
- On a German Windows Server 2019 (17763.3650, de-DE) the simplified guide chapter 1 of 2026-11-21 gives the expected step 7 result (0, 0, 7 times True, 48, viostor, no persistent default route): the network switched to DHCP with the two values of the adapter settings (stood in by netsh set address and set dnsservers source=dhcp) removes the static default route, and the step 6 block that writes the registry file, runs it as SYSTEM in a scheduled task and prints LastTaskResult in one paste returns 0. (KB-3SIE, V3 live, checked 2026-11-21)
- Up to three data-disk images can be registered in one IMS import. (KB-3WRH, V1 docs, checked 2026-06-12)
- A recorded run created a system-disk image from an ACTIVE ECS with a root disk of about 60 GB in roughly 6.5 minutes in eu-de. (KB-475G, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-05-22)
- hw_firmware_type of an active private image can be changed in place: PATCH /v2/cloudimages/{image_id} with Content-Type application/openstack-images-v2.1-json-patch and [{"op":"replace","path":"/hw_firmware_type","value":"bios"}] answers 200 and the image then reads bios; the same back to uefi works; no new import is needed when a VHDX was registered with the wrong firmware. (KB-4BBP, V3 live, checked 2026-11-13)
- A fixed VHD system disk of a Hyper-V Generation 1 Windows Server 2019 VM (150 GB) converted on the source side with Convert-VHD -VHDType Dynamic into a VHDX (51 s, file 10.5 GB) imports directly with POST /v2/cloudimages/action and hw_firmware_type bios: image active in 511 s, zvhd2, so the refusal of a large fixed file is avoided when the disk holds little data. (KB-4IN5, V3 live, checked 2026-11-11)
- Adding a support tag to an image needs the glance-native PATCH /v2/images/{id} with Content-Type application/openstack-images-v2.1-json-patch. PATCH /v2/cloudimages/{id} with application/json returns IMG.0001. (KB-4OZA, V3 live, checked 2026-07-21)
