- An OBS object moves to the Warm storage class at the earliest 30 days after its last update, so the first 30 days are billed at Standard whatever the lifecycle policy says. (KB-GV7Z, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-02)
- A copy between two OBS buckets in the same region is executed inside OBS. The client reports a transferred size of 0B while thousands of objects are copied, and no object data passes through the client network. (KB-GXBI, V3 live, checked 2026-06-17)
- A verified S3-compatible remote state backend against the OBS endpoint in eu-de has credential validation, region validation, the account-id request, the metadata API check and the S3 checksum all skipped. (KB-GY5W, V3 live, checked 2026-07-17)
- AWS DataSync in Enhanced mode moves data from S3 to third-party object storage without an agent; the agent and the rule that both locations sit in the agent Region apply to Basic mode only. An Enhanced task fails against a target that does not support object tagging unless the tag option is set. (KB-HDCR, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-01)
- Setting request_checksum_calculation to when_required stops the OBS body corruption. It works as the environment variable AWS_REQUEST_CHECKSUM_CALCULATION, as a key in a botocore Config object, or as the same key in the AWS CLI config file. obsutil is unaffected and wrote the 9-byte body correctly in the same run. (KB-HEFL, V3 live, checked 2026-07-28)
- WORM retention can be switched on for an OBS bucket that already exists, verified by a live call. The user manual states the opposite and is wrong on this point. (KB-HHVN, V3 live, checked 2026-09-01)
- An OBS multi-object delete request requires the Content-MD5 header, holding the base64 encoding of the raw MD5 digest of the XML request body. (KB-HIW3, V3 live, checked 2026-07-09)
- The OBS website endpoint name resolves differently depending on the resolver: from inside a VPC it returns an internal platform service address, from the internet a public address, for the same name. (KB-HJVH, V3 live, checked 2026-07-17)
- The Swift-compatible object interface of OBS honours If-None-Match: * on upload and answers HTTP 412 when the object already exists. It is a different API surface from the S3-compatible one used for S3-style presigned URLs. (KB-HMN7, V1 docs, checked 2026-06-26)
- The DLI.0007 error for CREATE TABLE ... USING on OBS tables lists PARQUET, ORC, JSON, CSV, HUDI and DELTA as supported formats, while the DLI documentation lists CSV, Parquet, ORC, JSON and Avro. Creating a HUDI or DELTA table itself is untested. (KB-I4YA, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- OpenTofu 1.12.6 reads a state file written by Terraform 1.16 (state format 4) without error. (KB-I6S4, V3 live, checked 2026-09-17)
- When a bucket policy lists domain/{domain_id}:root in NotPrincipal, OBS returns that entry as domain/{domain_id} on a GET of the policy. (KB-IF4S, V3 live, checked 2026-09-23)
- An S3 client that sends an implicit CreateBucket before uploading receives InvalidRequest with the message that header x-amz-bucket-object-lock-enabled must be 'true'. Writing into an already existing bucket succeeds. (KB-ILRB, V3 live, checked 2026-06-15)
- T Cloud Public (TCP) offers object storage for archive use in Object Storage Service (OBS): the Cold storage class is meant for data accessed about once a year, such as archives and long-term backups, lifecycle rules transition objects to the Warm or Cold class or delete them, and WORM retention in compliance mode keeps object versions from being overwritten or deleted by anyone for a set period. Cold objects are restored before they are read (KB-TA3K). (KB-JMBA, V1 docs, checked 2026-10-11)
- Versioning is refused on an OBS parallel file system, and Object Lock requires versioning first, so WORM retention does not run on a parallel file system. (KB-JP4L, V3 live, checked 2026-07-29)
- Restoring a Cold (GLACIER) OBS object with the Standard retrieval tier took about one to five hours in the observed runs. (KB-JRLG, V3 live, checked 2026-02-04)
- AWS SDKs running the 2025 default checksum mode upload objects to OBS with aws-chunked framing written inside the object body, and the upload reports success. (KB-K4AE, V3 live, checked 2026-09-15)
- Object Storage Service (OBS) is a cloud-native object storage service in which the customer creates buckets and stores and manages objects in them, through the console, the API or the S3 protocol. It is offered in the storage classes Standard, Warm and Cold, and it stores data redundantly across three availability zones. The Regions and Endpoints page lists API endpoints for OBS in eu-de and eu-nl. (KB-KD7R, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-10-14)
- An account including all its IAM users can create at most 100 buckets and parallel file systems together. By default there is no limit on the capacity or on the number of objects in a bucket. (KB-KUBY, V0 recheck due, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- The OBS API reference writes bucket notification events without a prefix, ObjectCreated:Put, Post, Copy, CompleteMultipartUpload or ObjectCreated:*, and ObjectRemoved:Delete, DeleteMarkerCreated or ObjectRemoved:*, and puts only TopicConfiguration elements into NotificationConfiguration. The sample of the S3-compatible PUT Bucket Notification page writes s3:ObjectCreated:* instead. (KB-LIWX, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- An OBS bucket whose default SSE-KMS encryption uses a custom CMK returns AccessDenied with "No privilege to this kms operation" to applications whose IAM group holds no KMS permission. Granting KMS CMKFullAccess at project level in eu-de to that group clears it. (KB-LJFS, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-03-07)
- In an OBS bucket notification configuration only TopicConfiguration is stored. A body with QueueConfiguration returns HTTP 200 while the configuration is not kept, and the notification does not fire. (KB-LP7I, V3 live, checked 2026-08-27)
- obsutil reads credentials from its own configuration file, so after an access key rotation every call returns HTTP 403 with error code InvalidAccessKeyId until obsutil config is run again with the new key. (KB-M3YL, V3 live, checked 2026-08-25)
- customdomain is a signed OBS subresource: a request that omits it from the string to sign is rejected as a signature mismatch. (KB-MKEY, V3 live, checked 2026-07-17)
- A completed obsutil rm prints output that omits the word succeed, so a success check grepping for that word reports a failure on a delete that worked. Listing the target confirms the delete. (KB-ML34, V3 live, checked 2026-08-25)
- OBS in eu-de accepts an upload-part-copy (x-obs-copy-source with x-obs-copy-source-range) of exactly 5368709120 bytes per part, and the part ETag equals the MD5 of the copied range, so a large object can be assembled byte-exactly from smaller uploaded objects and one reused all-zero object. (KB-NB4B, V3 live, checked 2026-11-11)
- The File number that obsutil ls prints is capped by its default -limit of 1000, so for a prefix holding more objects the printed count is lower than the real one. (KB-NJJU, V3 live, checked 2026-06-17)
- Completing an OBS multipart upload returns 400 Bad Request when the merged object would exceed 48.8 TB or when a part other than the last one is smaller than 100 KB. (KB-NRLY, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- A new IAM user whose group held the system role OBS Administrator of type AX at domain level still got 403 on HEAD bucket and on ListBuckets for 30 minutes after the grant. (KB-NRZ6, V3 live, checked 2026-09-23)
- OBS in eu-de accepts object writes signed with the OBS-specific (S3-style) signature, while the same writes signed over the API gateway signature path (SDK-HMAC-SHA256) still fail. (KB-OL6L, V3 live, checked 2026-10-03)
- OBS answers S3 Signature V4 requests from third-party clients over HTTPS at the regional endpoint, signing region eu-de. A hand-rolled SigV4 client got HTTP 200 on ListBuckets, on path-style and virtual-hosted bucket listings, and on the ?location= subresource, which returned eu-de. (KB-ORXG, V3 live, checked 2026-09-15)
- An IAM user's console session counts as the principal domain/{domain_id}:user/{user_id} of a bucket policy: uploading an object through the console worked on a bucket whose policy denies every action to NotPrincipal that one user and allows that user. (KB-OUOG, V3 live, checked 2026-09-23)
- An OBS bucket is created with a PUT to the bucket root carrying a CreateBucketConfiguration body with Location eu-de and Content-Type application/xml, which answers HTTP 200. A DELETE to the same bucket root after all objects are removed answers HTTP 204. (KB-OWQ7, V3 live, checked 2026-06-12)
- The S3-compatible bucket notification call against OBS in eu-de returned InvalidArgument, Unable to validate the following destination configurations, when the destination was an SMN topic urn. The SMN topic itself had been created with 201 in the same run. (KB-OXT6, V3 live, checked 2026-07-29)
- An OBS bucket listing returns a Size element per object together with IsTruncated. The next page is requested with max-keys and marker set to the last Key of the previous page, so total bucket size can be summed from the listings. (KB-OYZJ, V3 live, checked 2026-09-16)
- An OBS object uploaded with public-read is downloaded anonymously with Invoke-WebRequest from a Windows Server 2019 guest on the internet through its virtual-hosted URL, with the size and SHA-256 unchanged. (KB-P2AH, V3 live, checked 2026-11-03)
- OBS does not accept requests signed with the API gateway scheme SDK-HMAC-SHA256: a bucket listing signed that way answered HTTP 400 InvalidArgument, 'Authorization header is invalid -- one and only one ' ' (space) required'. OBS takes S3-style signatures (SigV2 or SigV4) or its own OBS scheme only. (KB-PHQ5, V3 live, checked 2026-10-05)
- During an OBS delta sync a NoSuchKey error appears for an object that was deleted between the listing call and the copy call. It reflects a source-side delete, not a failed copy. (KB-PQYE, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- OBS objects in the GLACIER storage class need a restore to STANDARD before a copy or an encryption run can read them. (KB-PWBT, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- A single OBS object can be up to 48.8 TB when it is uploaded with OBS Browser+, the API or an SDK. Through the API this size needs multipart upload because a single PUT or POST upload takes at most 5 GB. (KB-PWSD, V0 recheck due, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- Uploading a large directory to OBS with obsutil from a 4 vCPU ECS in eu-de lost 3 of 81 parts to TCP drops at parallelism 16. The same upload at parallelism 8 finished with no failed parts. (KB-QDQE, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-05-01)
- A multipart upload to OBS is limited to 10000 parts of 100 KB to 5 GB each (only the last part may be smaller), so the part size must be at least the object size divided by 10000. A 142 GiB object at 128 MiB parts uses 1136 parts. (KB-QLRN, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- OBS object-lock retention covers only objects uploaded after the policy is set, and uploading the same key again creates a new current version that sits outside the protection. (KB-R7FC, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-01)
- A bucket-level OBS request must go to the endpoint of the bucket's own region: a bucket created in eu-de returned 404 NoSuchBucket through the eu-nl endpoint. ListBuckets answered through either regional endpoint. (KB-RLOL, V3 live, checked 2026-09-15)
- On TCP, OBS parallel file systems do not support server-side encryption: the PFS feature guide (release 2026-06-23) lists it as a constraint and marks the bucket encryption APIs as not compatible. For SSE-KMS, OBS bucket default encryption takes SSEAlgorithm kms with an optional KMSMasterKeyID and ProjectID and has no BucketKeyEnabled setting. (KB-RM33, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- OBS V2 signing includes the query sub-resource in the canonical resource string. A request to GET /?storageinfo signed without it returns HTTP 403 SignatureDoesNotMatch in both path and virtual-host style, while a plain listing of the same bucket with the same key succeeds. (KB-RT73, V3 live, checked 2026-09-16)
- OBS error codes show where a temporary AK/SK fails: a wrong secret key gives 403 SignatureDoesNotMatch (the access key is known), a missing or changed x-obs-security-token gives 403 InvalidAccessKeyId, an unknown access key gives 403 InvalidAccessKeyId, and a known credential without rights gives 403 AccessDenied. (KB-RUGO, V3 live, checked 2026-10-28)
- An upload of about 400 GB spread over 81 objects, sent from an ECS to an OBS bucket in the same region, took roughly 65 minutes. (KB-S4QM, V3 live, checked 2026-05-01)
- OBS bucket notification event names are written without the s3: prefix. ObjectCreated:* is the accepted form. (KB-SAM5, V3 live, checked 2026-08-27)
- Copying 81 files of about 5 GB each to OBS with `obsutil cp -r -p 16` from a 4 vCPU host failed on 3 files with `write tcp: use of closed network connection` and exit code 8. The targeted retries with `-p 8` finished without a failure. (KB-SD7O, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-05-01)
- An OBS bucket without a bucket policy is closed to other accounts by its private ACL only. Inside the owning account every IAM user whose IAM permissions allow OBS reaches it, because ACLs do not control the IAM users of that account. Only an explicit Deny in a bucket policy overrides such an IAM allow. (KB-SPIK, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-23)
- A WORM-protected OBS object can have its storage class changed by a lifecycle rule while the retention lock still blocks deletion of the object. (KB-SQDE, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-02)
- OBS Cold storage restore has three documented speeds: Expedited 1 to 5 minutes, Standard 3 to 5 hours and Bulk 5 to 12 hours. (KB-SSWP, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-02)
- The system-managed obs/default key, like evs/default, cannot be granted to another tenant. Either the data is decrypted first or a custom CMK is used. (KB-T4BR, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- An OBS object in the Cold (GLACIER) class has to be restored to STANDARD before it can be read, copied or re-encrypted. The S3-compatible restore request takes a number of days and a retrieval tier; a HEAD on the object reports Restore ongoing-request="false" once the copy is ready. (KB-TA3K, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- On OBS, WORM retention on a bucket and cross-region replication of that bucket are mutually exclusive, per the OBS documentation. (KB-TA6I, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-01)
- The default storage class of an OBS bucket is set with PUT on the bucket URL with the ?storageClass sub-resource and an XML StorageClass body naming STANDARD, WARM or COLD, and read back with GET ?storageClass. The IAM action is obs:bucket:PutBucketStoragePolicy. (KB-TD5E, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- A PUT of an OBS object with the header x-obs-acl public-read stores the object with a read grant for everyone, shown by GET on the object with the acl query, while the bucket stays private. The ETag equals the MD5 of the body. An anonymous download itself was not checked in this call. (KB-TNTK, V3 live, checked 2026-11-03)
- Object Storage is billed with a cumulative graduated price: the price scales are filled one after another and each part of the used quantity is billed at its own scale. Taking the single tier a volume lands in and multiplying the whole volume by that rate over-states the bill. (KB-TPA7, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-08-27)
- An IAM user reaches one OBS state bucket through a bucket policy naming the principal as domain/<domain_id>:user/<user_id>, with no OBS role assigned at all. A second bucket in the same account stayed denied for that user. (KB-TQIX, V3 live, checked 2026-09-17)
- OBS Bucket is a resource type supported by enterprise projects. Buckets can be placed in an enterprise project and a user group authorized with an OBS role scoped to that enterprise project. (KB-UGXP, V1 docs, checked 2026-07-09)
- The S3-compatible API of OBS does not honour the conditional If-None-Match header on PUT, so a PUT overwrites an existing object. For create-only writes, check with HEAD first or turn on versioning so an earlier version is kept. (KB-UHIV, V3 live, checked 2026-07-17)
- Throughput between an ECS and OBS is bound by the EVS volume when another heavy job uses the same disk: a copy sharing one volume ran at 0.37 MiB/s, while the same OBS path on an idle instance ran at about 237 MiB/s. More EIP bandwidth does not change this. (KB-UKAC, V3 live, checked 2026-06-19)
- OBS accepts the native V2 signature for object keys with spaces, brackets and umlauts when the key is percent-encoded the same way in the request path and in the canonical resource of the string to sign. (KB-UPS3, V3 live, checked 2026-09-24)
- While a large file is uploaded with multipart (for example by OBS Browser+), ListObjects of the bucket shows no object, but GET /{bucket}?uploads lists the incomplete upload and GET /{bucket}?storageinfo counts the parts already uploaded in Size (ObjectNumber stays 0), so two storageinfo reads a few minutes apart give the upload rate. (KB-UV4A, V3 live, checked 2026-11-13)
- To pin a specific custom CMK as the bucket default encryption on OBS, the PUT ?encryption body carries KMSMasterKeyID inside ApplyServerSideEncryptionByDefault next to SSEAlgorithm kms. Without that element the default key obs/default is used, and the documented success response is HTTP 200. (KB-VOUX, V0 recheck due, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- OBS objects in the Cold storage class have to be restored before their content can be read. A bucket wide SSE-KMS re-encryption pass therefore triggers a restore for every cold object before it can rewrite it. (KB-VP4L, V3 live, checked 2026-02-04)
- An OBS bucket policy cannot protect itself inside its own account. An IAM user with object-storage rights in the owning account, not named in the policy, got 403 on HEAD, list, read, write, delete and ACL read under a Deny for every action to NotPrincipal one user, yet read the policy (200), replaced it (204) and deleted it (204), even when a second Deny named GetBucketPolicy, PutBucketPolicy and DeleteBucketPolicy. (KB-VSBB, V3 live, checked 2026-09-23)
- PUT /BUCKET?lifecycle on OBS answers 400 InvalidRequest Missing required header for this request: Content-MD5 without that header; with Content-MD5 of the XML body it answers 200 and the rule with Transition WARM and COLD and Expiration reads back. (KB-VUGB, V3 live, checked 2026-11-20)
- A client-side CRR copy reads objects in the STANDARD storage class. Objects in the Cold storage class have to be restored before a copy can read them. (KB-VV4F, V3 live, checked 2026-02-04)
- The OBS console uploads up to 100 files per batch with at most 5 GB in total and a single file there is also capped at 5 GB. For larger files the docs point to OBS Browser+ or multipart upload through the SDKs and APIs. (KB-W2R3, V0 recheck due, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- Actions in an OBS bucket policy use plain S3 names without a service prefix, such as ListBucket, GetObject and PutObject. The resource is the bucket name for bucket-level actions and bucket/* for objects, so a write can be narrowed to bucket/prefix/*. (KB-W3AJ, V3 live, checked 2026-06-17)
- The Content-Type header is part of the OBS V2 string to sign. A PUT whose Content-Type is added to the header set after the signature is computed returns HTTP 403, because the header travels with the request but is outside the signature. (KB-WDDP, V3 live, checked 2026-06-10)
- A Windows Server 2022 ECS without EIP and without NAT gateway in a VPC subnet with the default DNS 100.125.4.25 resolves obs.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com to an internal 100.125.x address and gets HTTPS answers from OBS (HTTP 405 on the endpoint, 403 on a private bucket), while https://www.microsoft.com times out, so OBS is usable from servers without internet access. (KB-WG2X, V3 live, checked 2026-11-12)
- SSE-S3 (AES256) on OBS maps to SSE-OBS rather than to SSE-KMS, confirmed per object and as a bucket default on a standard bucket. (KB-WGCN, V3 live, checked 2026-07-28)
- An OBS parallel file system rejects SSE-OBS and accepts SSE-KMS when the request supplies a bucket key and an explicit key id. (KB-WI66, V3 live, checked 2026-07-30)
- The OBS documentation asks for virtual-hosted-style requests when a request carries a bucket name, and states that virtual-hosted HTTPS fails certificate verification for bucket names containing a dot. Path-style requests still answered HTTP 200 in a live eu-de test. (KB-WQBX, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-15)
- Bucket default encryption on an OBS parallel file system applies only to objects written after it is set. Data already written stays unencrypted, and a freshly created parallel file system picks up the default cleanly. (KB-WS7V, V3 live, checked 2026-07-29)
- An OBS bucket policy in eu-de that denies ListBucket to every principal (Principal ID "*") also refuses the owning account: its ListBucket answers 403 AccessDenied. The owner can still delete the bucket (204). (KB-WXLI, V3 live, checked 2026-10-12)
- If an SMN topic used for OBS event notifications is deleted or loses its authorization for OBS, the OBS user guide warns that its subscribers may stop getting messages and that the event notification rules tied to the topic may be cleared automatically. (KB-WYFA, V0 recheck due, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- A presigned OBS GET URL gives time-limited read access to one object without handing over the secret key, while the URL itself carries the creator's access key ID, the Expires time and the signature. The link stops working when the expiry passes or when the object is deleted. (KB-WZOI, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- On TCP in eu-de an OBS multi-object delete request (POST /?delete) accepts at most 1000 object keys. Emptying a bucket with more objects takes one request per batch of that size. (KB-XUBF, V3 live, checked 2026-07-09)
- PUT /?notification on a bucket answers 400 InvalidArgument for an unsupported event, a wrong or unknown topic URN, a topic in another region than the bucket or overlapping filter rules, and 403 AccessDenied when the caller is neither the bucket owner nor granted PutBucketNotification. (KB-XUL7, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- OBS can be reached from on-premises without an EIP through a Direct Connect virtual gateway into a VPC that holds a VPC Endpoint for OBS, with the documented OBS service CIDR routed to the Direct Connect gateway. Traffic then stays off the internet gateway. (KB-XUWV, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-02)
- An OBS presigned URL uses V2 query-string auth with the parameters AccessKeyId, Expires and Signature, signing method, expiry and resource path with HMAC-SHA1. The resource in the string to sign must carry the URL-encoded object key; the raw key returns HTTP 403 for keys containing spaces. (KB-XZYG, V3 live, checked 2026-06-11)
- A PUT on the target key with the header x-obs-copy-source: /{bucket}/{percent-encoded source key} copies an object inside the bucket without the data leaving the object storage; the header is signed as an x-obs header in the V2 string to sign. (KB-YTYI, V3 live, checked 2026-09-24)
- While a restored OBS Cold object is available, the object is billed twice: once as the Cold object and once as the restored copy, which appears as "OBS Cold Object Storage Buffer". (KB-Z2QP, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-02)
- The OBS user guides make a KMS permission a prerequisite for SSE-KMS uploads and downloads: KMS Administrator in the region, or a custom policy allowing kms:dek:crypto, kms:dek:create, kms:cmk:get, kms:cmk:list, kms:cmk:generate and kms:cmk:crypto. The OBS Browser guide sets this prerequisite in a flow that encrypts with the default key obs/default. (KB-ZD3W, V0 recheck due, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- A recursive obsutil copy recreates the source folder name under the destination prefix by default, so the result is nested one level deeper. The -flat option places the contents directly under the destination prefix. (KB-ZE54, V3 live, checked 2026-06-17)

## iam (150)

- Moving a resource to another enterprise project does not change the resource itself, but it changes who can access it when access is granted per enterprise project: a user who holds permissions only for the source enterprise project can lose access after the move. (KB-27UC, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- On TCP the default IAM quota is 500 users per tenant. GET /v3.0/OS-QUOTA/domains/{domain_id} returned quota 500, min 50 and max 2000 for type user on two test tenants in eu-de on 2026-10-02. (KB-2DEG, V3 live, checked 2026-10-02)
- The OIDC id_token exchange on TCP requires iss and aud in the JWT (without one: 400 IAM.0007) but not iat, nbf, exp or jti: a JWT without any one of these four was exchanged with 201, a JWT without exp too. A JWT with nbf or iat 10 minutes in the future is refused with 400 IAM.0007, and exp 30 days ahead is accepted. (KB-2SDA, V3 live, checked 2026-10-27)
- Enterprise Project Management Service (EPS) groups cloud resources such as ECS, OBS and VPC into enterprise projects that match departments, business units or environments such as production and test, and gives a unified view of resource management and access control across services and regions. The documentation describes it as a cloud governance platform for managing personnel, resources and permissions by the organizational structure. (KB-2U4K, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-10-14)
- With an AK/SK-signed request in eu-de, GET /v3/projects on iam.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com lists the projects and sub-projects of the account, while GET /v3/users and GET /v3/groups answer HTTP 403 'You are not authorized to perform the requested action.' A key that can list projects therefore cannot prove whether IAM users or groups exist. (KB-33GL, V3 live, checked 2026-10-04)
- The oidc protocol sub-resource under an IAM identity provider carries the mapping_id of the federation mapping in its request body. (KB-364P, V3 live, checked 2026-08-27)
- On the check date GET /v3/roles with per_page=300 returned about 220 system roles, each with its policy document. GET /v3.0/OS-ROLE/roles returned only the tenant's own custom policies, so system roles and custom policies come from different listings. (KB-3AAE, V3 live, checked 2026-08-19)
- The console forces a password change at the first login of a new user, who shows pwd_status false until then. After that change the password an automation still holds no longer authenticates. (KB-3JTS, V3 live, checked 2026-08-19)
- On a project whose router quota is full, POST /v1/{project_id}/vpcs with the temporary AK/SK or the project-scoped token of a federated identity whose group holds only Tenant Guest returned 409 VPC.0114 OverQuota, not 403. The quota answer hides the permission answer, so a deny test for a VPC create needs a project with free VPC quota; a group with VPC ReadOnlyAccess got 403 in the same run. (KB-3NUN, V3 live, checked 2026-10-28)
- OBS does not take an IAM token in X-Auth-Token: the bucket list answered 403 "Anonymous access is forbidden" for the administrator token too. Temporary AK/SK of a Tenant Guest group pass the OBS signature but get 403 Access Denied on the bucket list, so OBS needs its own OBS role. (KB-3NXA, V3 live, checked 2026-10-26)
- The OIDC id_token exchange POST /v3.0/OS-AUTH/id-token/tokens returns 201 for every scope form: none (an unscoped token with no roles and an empty catalog), a project by id, a project by name and the domain by id or by name. (KB-3NYK, V3 live, checked 2026-10-26)
- Enterprise Project Service accepts a domain-scoped federated token from the OIDC id_token exchange, and refuses project-scoped federated tokens and every temporary AK/SK with 401 EPS.0003. (KB-3QWM, V3 live, checked 2026-10-26)
- Identity and Access Management (IAM) is the central service that manages users, user groups and their permissions to T Cloud Public (TCP) resources and services, and controls who (users, groups or federated identities) may access which service and resource under which conditions, such as time, IP address or MFA. The documentation adds federated single sign-on from the customer's identity system, delegation of access to another account or a cloud service, and security policies for multi-factor authentication, login authentication, passwords and access control lists. The Regions and Endpoints page lists API endpoints for IAM in eu-de and eu-nl. (KB-424Q, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-10-14)
- A federated identity whose only role is VPC ReadOnlyAccess lists ECS servers: GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/detail returns 200 with the servers, the same as the administrator. The role grants it: the system role VPC ReadOnlyAccess (type XA) lists ecs:*:get*, ecs:*:list*, vpc:*:get and vpc:*:list. ECS does check: a federated identity with no role gets 403 Ecs.0110 (a TCP test tenant, 2026-10-27). (KB-43SS, V3 live, checked 2026-10-27)
- The IAM federation API uses two spellings: v3 paths use identity_providers with an underscore, v3.0 paths use identity-providers with a hyphen. (KB-46TQ, V3 live, checked 2026-08-27)
- The MRS create-cluster API accepts MRS_ECS_DEFAULT_AGENCY even before that IAM agency exists and provisions it. Creating an agency directly needs identity:createAgencyEx, which Security Administrator holds and a normal AK/SK user lacks. (KB-47IL, V3 live, checked 2026-07-28)
- The OIDC configuration of one identity provider is read with GET /v3.0/OS-FEDERATION/identity-providers/{idp_id}/openid-connect-config. It answers HTTP 200 with access_mode, either program or program_console, idp_url as the issuer, client_id as the audience and signing_key. (KB-47JM, V3 live, checked 2026-07-02)
- Six fine-grained system roles on the eu-de project are enough to build a VPC + NAT + ELB v3 + ECS stack with OpenTofu: ECS FullAccess, VPC FullAccess (which covers EIPs and security groups), ELB FullAccess, NAT FullAccess, EVS FullAccess and IMS ReadOnlyAccess. (KB-4EVM, V3 live, checked 2026-09-17)
- POST /v1.0/enterprise-projects/{id}/resources/filter with resource_types and projects in the body returns 200 and lists the resources held by that enterprise project. Region-level resources are returned only when the projects field is set. (KB-4OYO, V3 live, checked 2026-06-02)
- An AK/SK-signed GET https://iam.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com/v3/projects returns HTTP 200 with the project list when the key is valid, which tests a key in one call without creating anything. (KB-4SSK, V4 kept live, checked 2026-08-18)
- With the OBS V2 signer that works for an IAM user's temporary AK/SK, temporary AK/SK of a federated identity got 403 AccessDenied on OBS while its OBS roles were granted on the domain only. A real IAM user with the same domain-only grant got the same 403, so the refusal comes from the grant scope: grant OBS roles for all resources (KB-ZHEX). (KB-52DA, V3 live, checked 2026-10-28)
- An enterprise project of TCP cannot be deleted by the tenant, neither in the console nor through the API: it can only be disabled. A disabled enterprise project still counts against the enterprise project quota and cannot be modified. Only the operator's O&M can enable deletion for a tenant, on a support request. (KB-57EM, V1 docs, checked 2026-10-06)
- The signing key of an OIDC identity provider is held as a stored static JWKS in the provider configuration. The platform does not fetch the issuer jwks_uri at exchange time, so a key rotation at the external provider breaks the token exchange until the stored key is refreshed. (KB-5CFW, V3 live, checked 2026-07-02)
- A token from POST /v3/auth/tokens is returned in the X-Subject-Token response header and has a validity period of 24 hours, but it becomes invalid within 30 minutes after the IAM user is deleted or disabled or its password, access key or permissions change. (KB-5FOB, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- Deleting an OIDC identity provider with DELETE /v3/OS-FEDERATION/identity_providers/{id} also removes its mapping: a DELETE of the mapping right after it returns 404 Could not find mapping. Seen on two tenants. (KB-5LGF, V3 live, checked 2026-10-27)
- Microsoft: Entra ID group claims have a five-group limit when the token is issued through the implicit flow; a user in more than five groups gets hasgroups true and no groups claim. The TCP IAM OpenID Connect identity provider asks for response type id_token by default, so a conversion rule on the groups claim needs the Entra option Groups assigned to the application to keep the list at five or fewer. (KB-6D2W, V1 docs, checked 2026-11-21)
- POST /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/credentials with a user_id in the body returns a credential object holding the access key and the secret key. The secret appears in that response only. (KB-6H6V, V3 live, checked 2026-06-17)
- The current OIDC signing key of an identity provider is returned by GET /v3.0/OS-FEDERATION/identity-providers/{idp_id}/openid-connect-config. The same path under /v3 answers HTTP 404 APIGW.0101 with no configuration, so a caller that ignores the status sees no key and rotates on every run. (KB-6LSL, V3 live, checked 2026-09-25)
- GET /v3/OS-FEDERATION/identity_providers on the IAM endpoint answers HTTP 200 and lists the identity providers configured for the account, each with its description text. (KB-6MBW, V4 kept live, checked 2026-07-02)
- GET /v3.0/OS-QUOTA/projects/{project_id} on the eu-de region project returns the quota of IAM subprojects in that region as type project with quota 10, min 2 and max 50 and the count used; the domain quota response GET /v3.0/OS-QUOTA/domains/{domain_id} does not carry the project type. (KB-6YPN, V3 live, checked 2026-11-10)
- In TCP IAM a user with management console access only gets no API token: a password token request (POST /v3/auth/tokens) is refused with HTTP 403. With programmatic access ticked as well, the same user name and password get a token and read the APIs; no access key has to be created for that. (KB-6ZJC, V3 live, checked 2026-10-23)
- A new IAM user added to existing groups that already carry object-storage rights reached a bucket at once: HEAD bucket answered 200 on the first try, seconds after the group membership was set. (KB-736M, V3 live, checked 2026-09-23)
- The OIDC id_token exchange on TCP takes RS256 only among the tested algorithms: RS512 and PS256 with a key the JWKS holds are refused with 400 IAM.0007, as are alg none and HS256. An RSA JWK without an alg field works for RS256. A JWT header without kid is refused with 400 IAM.0007 when the JWKS holds two keys. (KB-76LF, V3 live, checked 2026-10-27)
- A FunctionGraph function configured with an agency that is delegated to a different service is refused with FSS.1501 "The delegate not exist". The agency has to be delegated to FunctionGraph itself. (KB-774P, V3 live, checked 2026-07-30)
- Temporary AK/SK of a federated workload from the OIDC id_token exchange work on OBS when the mapped group holds an OBS role for all resources (inherited_to_projects): bucket list, HEAD bucket, list objects, get and put object returned 200 at once with OBS OperateAccess, OBS Administrator or Tenant Administrator, from a project-scoped and from a domain-scoped federated token. (KB-7D5K, V3 live, checked 2026-10-28)
- OpenStack projects (OP) group and isolate the compute, storage and network resources of one tenant into separate projects, and each project can be assigned to users and to user groups. (KB-7IGI, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-10-14)
- The Config resource recorder needs an IAM agency that trusts the service domain op_svc_rms, created with POST /v3.0/OS-AGENCY/agencies and the field trust_domain_name. The OBS writability check of the recorder appears to run under that service identity rather than under the caller. (KB-7XJY, V3 live, checked 2026-07-17)
- On T Cloud Public (TCP) in eu-de the Enterprise Project Service is domain-level: a request to /v1.0/enterprise-projects signed with AK/SK, or carrying a project-scoped IAM token, returns HTTP 401 with error code EPS.0003. A domain-scoped IAM token is accepted. (KB-A2NP, V3 live, checked 2026-06-02)
- The TCP IAM role OBS Buckets Viewer is of type XA and lists S3-style actions (s3:GetBucketLocation, s3:HeadBucket, s3:ListAllMyBuckets, s3:ListBucket), while OBS ReadOnlyAccess is of type AX with obs:bucket actions. (KB-AGFZ, V3 live, checked 2026-11-10)
- The OIDC id_token exchange on TCP does not insist on an ID token shape: a JWT with header typ at+jwt and the claims scope and client_id returned 201, and so did a JWT whose aud is an array holding the configured client_id. (KB-AI2P, V3 live, checked 2026-10-26)
- After an access key is deleted in IAM, a signed API call made with that key returns HTTP 401. (KB-AJJH, V3 live, checked 2026-08-24)
- The IAM policy actions for the Enterprise Project Service are eps:enterpriseProjects:list, create, get, update, enable and disable, plus eps:resources:list, add and remove. (KB-AOXY, V1 docs, checked 2026-06-02)
- An IAM agency delegates permissions to another account on the same platform or to a platform service. Identity from another cloud or from an enterprise directory is integrated through an IAM identity provider with SAML 2.0 or OIDC. (KB-AVKS, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-01)
- Temporary AK/SK of a federated identity follow the permissions of the mapped group: with VPC ReadOnlyAccess on the region project, GET /v1/{project_id}/vpcs answers 200 and POST /v1/{project_id}/vpcs answers 403 "Policy doesn't allow vpc:vpcs:create to be performed". (KB-AWO2, V3 live, checked 2026-10-26)
- The IAM service catalogue (GET /v3/services) of a test tenant in eu-de still lists entries such as heat, Workspace, Data Ingestion Service and bms; a catalogue entry is no proof that a service is offered, which the service description decides. (KB-AYPC, V3 live, checked 2026-11-20)
- TCP IAM lists two system roles with the display name OBS ReadOnlyAccess, system_all_36 and system_all_30, both fine-grained type AX with the same four actions obs:bucket:ListAllMybuckets, HeadBucket, ListBucket and GetBucketLocation. A script that takes the first match of the display name grants only one of them. (KB-BGB5, V3 live, checked 2026-10-28)
- A FunctionGraph function gets credentials from an IAM agency delegated to the service account op_svc_cff and bound to the function. Inside the function, context.getToken() returns an agency-scoped IAM token for the X-Auth-Token header, so no access key or password is stored. (KB-BU5C, V3 live, checked 2026-07-17)
- Temporary AK/SK of a federated identity got 403 AccessDenied on OBS while its group held OBS Buckets Viewer or OBS ReadOnlyAccess on the domain only, 35 minutes after the grant. The cause is the grant, not the federation: a real IAM user in such a group is refused too, and the same roles granted for all resources (inherited_to_projects) work at once (KB-ZHEX). (KB-BZPD, V3 live, checked 2026-10-28)
- The OIDC id_token exchange on TCP does not reject a replayed token: the same JWT with the same jti exchanged twice returned 201 both times. (KB-BZVX, V3 live, checked 2026-10-26)
- With access_mode set to programmatic the console login form refuses the user while API access with a token and signed AK/SK calls keep working. GET /v3.0/OS-USER/users/{user_id} returns access_mode, is_domain_owner, xuser_type and last_pwd_auth_time. (KB-C273, V3 live, checked 2026-08-19)
- assume_role through POST /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/securitytokens works with a federated token from the OIDC exchange only when that token is domain-scoped; a project-scoped federated token is refused with 403 "X-Auth-Token is invalid". The identity needs Agent Operator on the domain, and an agency trusted to its own domain can be created. (KB-CCEB, V3 live, checked 2026-10-26)
- GET /v1.0/enterprise-projects/providers?limit=200 returned 200 with 46 supported services and total_count 46 on 2026-09-25, eu-de. Earlier checks showed 44 on 2026-06-02 and 43 on 2026-07-23, so the returned set changes between checks. (KB-COAJ, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- A read-only AK/SK in a tenant used by several teams returned the VPCs, subnets, servers and floating addresses created by all of them. An inventory built on the raw listings held 35 of 56 objects belonging to other teams. (KB-D4J4, V3 live, checked 2026-09-17)
- T Cloud Public (TCP) manages users, user groups and federation in Identity and Access Management (IAM): administrators create users for people, systems and applications, put them in groups that carry the permissions, and connect the enterprise identity provider for single sign-on via SAML (virtual users or IAM users) or OpenID Connect. The OIDC configuration of an identity provider was read live through the IAM federation API (KB-47JM). (KB-D64T, V1 docs, checked 2026-10-11)
- The EPS v1.0 API in eu-de offers list, create, query, update, enable and disable for an enterprise project. It exposes no delete operation, so an enterprise project can only be disabled. (KB-DACZ, V1 docs, checked 2026-06-02)
- GET /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/credentials/{ak} returns the owning user_id, the status and the create_time of an access key. (KB-DH33, V3 live, checked 2026-08-18)
- In the password authentication body of POST /v3/auth/tokens the user's domain goes under the key domain. Using the key domain_name instead produces HTTP 401, which reads like a wrong user name or password. (KB-DHCO, V3 live, checked 2026-07-29)
- Enterprise Project Service is a domain-level service in eu-de reached at https://eps.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com/v1.0/enterprise-projects. Its API accepts a domain-scoped IAM token only, sent as X-Auth-Token; AK/SK signing and project-scoped tokens are rejected with 401 EPS.0003. (KB-DHDH, V3 live, checked 2026-06-03)
- GET /v3/projects?name=eu-de on iam.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com returns 200 with a projects array holding the region project name and its id. The call is AK/SK-signed like any other, so a project id can be resolved without a token and without the password flow. (KB-EAG6, V4 kept live, checked 2026-08-18)
- An access key can return HTTP 200 on read calls across IMS, VPC, ECS, EVS, EIP, KMS and OBS and still fail on a create call. (KB-EP4A, V3 live, checked 2026-08-18)
- TMS and EPS calls made with project-scoped credentials return HTTP 401 in TCP. Both APIs accept domain-scoped credentials. (KB-EPXJ, V3 live, checked 2026-09-25)
- GET /v3/projects on iam.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com is the cheapest credential test. A live AK/SK returns 200 with the project list, a deleted or wrong key returns 401 APIGW.0301 "Incorrect IAM authentication information". (KB-FHGI, V4 kept live, checked 2026-08-18)
- IAM lists three system roles named OBS Administrator in eu-de, two of type XX and one of type AX. The type AX role accepted a domain-level group assignment: PUT /v3/domains/{domain_id}/groups/{group_id}/roles/{role_id} returned 204. (KB-FLAD, V3 live, checked 2026-09-23)
- On tms.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com the path GET /v1.0/predefined-tags does not exist: it answers 404 APIGW.0101 (API not published) for every credential. The documented path is GET /v1.0/predefine_tags; it reaches TMS, which answered a read-only key with 401 TMS.0003 Unauthorized user. (KB-GGE2, V4 kept live, checked 2026-10-27)
- An agency with OBS roles, taken on with assume_role in POST /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/securitytokens from a domain-scoped federated token of a group with Agent Operator, gives temporary AK/SK that list buckets and get and put objects on OBS (200). The same call with a project-scoped federated token returned 403 X-Auth-Token is invalid. (KB-GVRM, V3 live, checked 2026-10-28)
- IAM allows at most two access keys per user in eu-de. When both slots are in use, one existing key has to be deleted before a new one can be created. (KB-H5QL, V3 live, checked 2026-08-24)
- An IAM user on TCP holds at most two permanent access keys, and creating a third fails with HTTP 400 akSkNumExceed. At that cap one key is deleted before the new one is created while the other key stays valid, and the API reference lists a user token (X-Auth-Token) as mandatory for both key calls. (KB-HBZF, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- PUT /v3.0/OS-MFA/mfa-devices/bind and PUT /v3.0/OS-MFA/mfa-devices/unbind take a flat JSON body; a nested body returns 400 with the message Request body is invalid. A bind supplies two consecutive TOTP codes and answers 204. (KB-HMTS, V3 live, checked 2026-08-19)
- IAM user and credential calls need a password-based, domain-scoped token from POST iam/v3/auth/tokens, returned in the X-Subject-Token response header. POST /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/credentials then returns an access key and its secret, and the secret appears only in that response. (KB-HRK7, V3 live, checked 2026-06-17)
- GET /v3.0/OS-USER/users/{user_id}/login-protect returns 404 with error code IAM.0004 for a freshly created user whose login protection was never configured. (KB-HRNW, V3 live, checked 2026-08-19)
- TCP IAM lists two system roles with the display name OBS OperateAccess, system_all_37 and system_all_31, both type AX with bucket list and read actions plus object actions such as obs:object:DeleteObject and AbortMultipartUpload. (KB-HS7Z, V3 live, checked 2026-10-28)
- CTS GET /v3/{project_id}/trackers needs a project-scoped token: domain-scoped and unscoped tokens get 403 CTS.0013 "Invalid token scope, token scope is not project". (KB-I2LV, V3 live, checked 2026-10-26)
- The TCP IAM user guide gives the redirect URIs of an OpenID Connect identity provider only as the paths /authui/oidc/redirect and /authui/oidc/post; the host is missing on the page. Its table of federated logins lists virtual user SSO by OpenID Connect as SP-initiated only (IdP-initiated not supported), so the console logon starts from the login link of the identity provider. (KB-I3TZ, V1 docs, checked 2026-11-21)
- The OIDC configuration of a TCP identity provider does not take an EC key in signing_key: a JWKS with two RSA keys and one EC P-256 key (ES256) is refused with 400 IAM.0011 Request body is invalid, the same JWKS without the EC key is accepted. (KB-IJXM, V3 live, checked 2026-10-27)
- Temporary AK/SK from securitytokens method token (federated source token) need the header X-Project-Id in the signature for the VPC v1 API in eu-de: without it GET /v1/{project_id}/vpcs answers 401 "Invalid token in the header", with it 200. GET /v3/projects on IAM works without the header. (KB-J5ZJ, V3 live, checked 2026-10-26)
- POST /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/securitytokens with method token and a federated token from the OIDC id_token exchange returns 201 for duration_seconds 900 and for 86400. (KB-JB2I, V3 live, checked 2026-10-26)
- DELETE /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/credentials/{access_key} answers 204 and revokes the key at once. The next signed call with that key returned 401 with error code APIGW.0301 saying the ak does not exist, so there is no grace period. (KB-JEWE, V3 live, checked 2026-08-24)
- A service validates a request before it authorises it. With a read-only key, a DELETE of a nonexistent id answered 404 and a POST with an empty body answered 400, so neither answer tells anything about the key's permissions. (KB-JNYO, V3 live, checked 2026-09-17)
- The service calls of a region-scoped deployment need the region's project id, which IAM returns for an AK/SK-signed GET /v3/projects?name=eu-de. (KB-JQOG, V4 kept live, checked 2026-08-18)
- GET /v3/auth/domains on the IAM endpoint returns the account domain id. That id is required as the domain_id query parameter of GET /v3.0/OS-AGENCY/agencies, which lists the agencies of the account. (KB-JXAY, V4 kept live, checked 2026-07-02)
- The OIDC id_token exchange on TCP accepts a JWT whose aud is an array that holds the client_id of the identity provider: POST /v3.0/OS-AUTH/id-token/tokens returned 201. (KB-JXY3, V3 live, checked 2026-10-27)
- The access key calls under /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL run with a domain-scoped token obtained from POST /v3/auth/tokens. (KB-JZJP, V3 live, checked 2026-08-24)
- The federated token from POST /v3.0/OS-AUTH/id-token/tokens carries expires_at one day after issued_at whatever the exp of the presented JWT. A JWT that expires 5 minutes after issue still yields a federated token valid for one day, so the JWT exp does not shorten the session. (KB-KS6Q, V3 live, checked 2026-10-26)
- GET /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/credentials/{access_key} returns the user_id that owns that access key, which maps a key to its IAM user without a lookup by user name. (KB-LC2D, V3 live, checked 2026-08-24)
- On TCP the IAM quota response lists enterprise project grants as types assigment_group_ep, assigment_user_ep and assigment_agency_ep with quota 500 each, and grants on multiple projects as assigment_group_mp and assigment_agency_mp with quota 200 each. Seen on two test tenants in eu-de on 2026-10-02. (KB-LC5E, V3 live, checked 2026-10-02)
- In the OIDC id_token exchange on TCP a JWT without sub, against a mapping rule that takes sub as the user name, returned 403 IAM.0002 You are not authorized to perform the requested action, not a 400. (KB-LN7B, V3 live, checked 2026-10-28)
- A federated identity from the OIDC id_token exchange cannot create a permanent access key: POST /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/credentials with its own federated token and the user id from that token returns 404 Could not find user, because the federated user is no IAM user. (KB-LPTP, V3 live, checked 2026-10-27)
- A read-only console key needs CCE ReadOnlyAccess to list clusters and nodes plus the ReadOnlyAccess role of every other service it reads. With only those roles a VPC create sent with a valid body was refused. (KB-LQ2Y, V3 live, checked 2026-09-17)
- Effective permissions are cumulative. IAM-project policies are evaluated before enterprise-project policies and the first Allow wins, so a narrow enterprise-project grant cannot cap a broader IAM grant held by the same user or group. (KB-LTXL, V1 docs, checked 2026-07-15)
- The default login policy locks an account after five failed attempts and keeps it locked for 15 minutes; requests then return 401 with the message The account is locked. (KB-M5XE, V3 live, checked 2026-08-19)
- Temporary AK/SK from POST /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/securitytokens with method token and the password token of an IAM user with Tenant Administrator list OBS buckets: GET / on OBS with the V2 signature and x-obs-security-token returned 200 at once. This is the positive control for the 403 of federated temporary AK/SK on OBS. (KB-MMI7, V3 live, checked 2026-10-28)
- POST /v3.0/OS-MFA/virtual-mfa-devices has to carry the token of the user the device is created for; the same call with an administrator token returns 403. The successful response holds base32_string_seed and serial_number. (KB-MSQX, V3 live, checked 2026-08-19)
- Refusals of the OIDC id_token exchange: wrong aud, wrong iss, an expired JWT and a JWT signed by a key not in the stored JWKS each return 400 IAM.0007 "Request parameter 'id token' is invalid"; a valid JWT that matches no conversion rule returns 403 IAM.0002. (KB-N2JY, V3 live, checked 2026-10-26)
- On TCP the default IAM quota is 128 custom policies per tenant. GET /v3.0/OS-QUOTA/domains/{domain_id} returned quota 128, min 128 and max 5000 for type policy on two test tenants in eu-de on 2026-10-02. (KB-N4IJ, V3 live, checked 2026-10-02)
- IAM management calls signed with an AK/SK pair, such as POST /v3/groups, return 403 with the message 'You are not authorized to perform the requested action' even for a member of the admin group, unless the request carries the X-Domain-Id header with the account id. With that header the same call returns 201. Reads of the caller's own credential and user work without it. (KB-N66Y, V3 live, checked 2026-09-23)
- POST /v3/auth/tokens accepts the identity methods password and totp together and returns 201 with mfa_authn_at in the body. Only verification_method vmfa gates this token API: with sms or email a password-only token request still returns 201, while vmfa answers 401 mfa totp code verify fail. (KB-NCCP, V3 live, checked 2026-08-19)
- With an AK/SK key of a tenant, GET /v3/auth/domains on IAM returns the account (domain) name and id, and GET /v3/auth/projects returns the region projects (eu-de, eu-nl) together with sub-projects such as MOS, each with its id; both are a quick read-only way to confirm which tenant a key belongs to before a write. (KB-NFTW, V3 live, checked 2026-11-13)
- IAM user passwords expire after about 90 days by default, which breaks integrations that authenticate with a user password. On OpenShift this shows up as HTTP 401 against EVS and stops Cinder CSI volume provisioning until the password and the stored credentials secret are rotated. (KB-NPZE, V1 docs, checked 2026-06-08)
- POST /v3.0/OS-AUTH/id-token/tokens accepts a JWT that carries no user claims at all (only iss, aud, sub, iat, nbf, exp, jti and one custom claim), signed RS256 with a key held in the static JWKS of an OIDC identity provider with access_mode program. It returns 201 with methods mapped and the user name and group from the conversion rule, so a non-human workload token works for the exchange. (KB-O6CG, V3 live, checked 2026-10-26)
- An IAM user gets enterprise project access from the Enterprise Management administrator policy, which is a global-service policy. The action names are eps:enterpriseProjects:* and eps:resources:*. (KB-OD7A, V1 docs, checked 2026-06-03)
- Per-user login protection is set with PUT /v3.0/OS-USER/users/{user_id}/login-protect and takes verification_method sms, email or vmfa. GET /v3.0/OS-USER/login-protects lists only users that were ever configured, and the value none was seen in that listing. (KB-ONOY, V3 live, checked 2026-08-19)
- A request signed with an access key that was deleted in IAM returns HTTP 401 with error_code APIGW.0301 and error_msg "Incorrect IAM authentication information: ak <key> not exist". The wording identifies a missing key, not a wrong signature. (KB-OQZL, V3 live, checked 2026-08-18)
- In the IAM role catalog the display_name carries the meaning: the role shown as OBS Administrator appears under system_all_34 and system_all_35, while a similar internal name such as system_all_64 belongs to an unrelated service. Matching is done on display_name. (KB-OWB7, V3 live, checked 2026-07-17)
- In the EPS providers listing total_count is not reliable with a small limit: at limit=5 the response reported total_count 4, while limit=200 returned the full set with a matching count. (KB-P7Z6, V3 live, checked 2026-06-02)
- POST /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/securitytokens with identity method token returns 201, but the response carries no scope information and a project-scoped endpoint called with that credential answers 400 Common.0018, tenantId in token is not the same with in URL, whatever scope the source token had. (KB-PADJ, V3 live, checked 2026-08-19)
- POST /v1.0/enterprise-projects/{id}/resources-migrate with resource_type, resource_id and project_id returns 204 and moves an existing resource into that enterprise project. Passing id 0 moves the resource back to the default enterprise project. The round trip was run with a VPC. (KB-PBX6, V3 live, checked 2026-06-03)
- A user with no IAM policy attached can still obtain a project-scoped token, but an EIP create call from that user returns HTTP 403 PolicyNotAuthorized. (KB-PKZG, V3 live, checked 2026-07-17)
- A signed IAM API call that returns HTTP 401 with error code APIGW.0301 and an ak not exist message points to an access key deleted in IAM, not to a signing error. (KB-PPUV, V3 live, checked 2026-08-18)
- DELETE /v3/users/{user_id} on the IAM endpoint answers HTTP 204 and removes the user together with the access keys issued to that user. (KB-QD7I, V3 live, checked 2026-06-17)
- A user created with POST /v3/users needs a few seconds before it can authenticate. A token request issued immediately returns 401 with the message The username or password is wrong, while a wait of about five seconds was enough. (KB-QEHQ, V3 live, checked 2026-08-19)
- PUT /v3.0/OS-USER/users/{user_id} with {"user":{"access_mode":"programmatic"}} answers 200 right after POST /v3/users, and an access key created with POST /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/credentials was usable about 10 seconds after creation. (KB-QO6I, V3 live, checked 2026-09-17)
- PUT /v3.0/OS-USER/users/{user_id} sets access_mode to console, programmatic or default. With console a password token request returns 403 saying the user only supports console access, and an access key is still created with 201 although it does not work. (KB-QP2C, V3 live, checked 2026-08-19)
- On TCP the v3.0 identity-providers path serves only the openid-connect-config sub-resource. List and get of identity-providers under /v3.0/OS-FEDERATION return 404 APIGW.0101, so providers are enumerated through the v3 path and OIDC is configured through v3.0. (KB-QWLC, V4 kept live, checked 2026-08-27)
- KMS in eu-de: create-key, encrypt-data, decrypt-data and create-datakey work on a customer key (AES_256, origin kms); create-key with origin external makes a key for imported material, and get-parameters-for-import with RSAES_OAEP_SHA_256 returns a wrapping public key and an import token; schedule-key-deletion with pending_days 7 sets key_state 4. (KB-R76D, V3 live, checked 2026-11-20)
- OIDC identity federation on TCP needs four calls in order: PUT /v3/OS-FEDERATION/identity_providers/{name}, PUT /v3/OS-FEDERATION/mappings/{id}, PUT /v3/OS-FEDERATION/identity_providers/{name}/protocols/oidc, then POST /v3.0/OS-FEDERATION/identity-providers/{name}/openid-connect-config. Skipping the protocol call makes the final call return 404 IAM.0004, "Could not find federated protocol oidc". (KB-REQO, V3 live, checked 2026-08-27)
- Signing a TCP API request needs both halves of the pair: the access key ID (AK) identifies the IAM user and travels in the signed request, while the secret access key (SK) is the secret that produces the signature. (KB-RGQ3, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- POST /v3/auth/tokens on the IAM endpoint returns the token in the X-Subject-Token response header. Later API calls send that value in the X-Auth-Token request header. (KB-S4NP, V3 live, checked 2026-07-29)
- T Cloud Public (TCP) supports multi-factor authentication in Identity and Access Management (IAM): users bind a virtual MFA device, an app that generates 6-digit TOTP codes, and login protection asks for an MFA, SMS or email code after the password. Token requests that combine password and TOTP were tested live (KB-NCCP). (KB-TFIN, V1 docs, checked 2026-10-11)
- Enabling the Config/RMS resource recorder takes two steps in order: an IAM agency trusted to the service account op_svc_rms is created first, then the tracker-config call turns the recorder on. (KB-TIBM, V3 live, checked 2026-07-17)
- An inline policy in POST /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/securitytokens with method token did not narrow the credential on non-OBS APIs: a policy allowing only VPC get and list still let the temporary AK/SK of a Tenant Guest group list EVS, IMS, ELB v3, RDS, CCE, SMN, CES, LTS, CTS, DNS and RMS. (KB-TLN2, V3 live, checked 2026-10-26)
- Microsoft: for SAML sign-on Entra ID creates a self-signed signing certificate per application that is valid for three years and sends a mail 60, 30 and 7 days before it expires. The TCP IAM user guide says that after a change of the metadata the latest metadata file of the enterprise identity provider must be uploaded again, so a SAML federation with Entra ID needs one manual step per certificate change and no scheduled job. (KB-TO5O, V1 docs, checked 2026-11-21)
- POST /v3/groups on a TCP account that has reached its user group quota returns 400 with the message The number of groups has reached the maximum! A script that creates groups must handle it, because shared test tenants can be at the limit. (KB-TPYH, V3 live, checked 2026-10-28)
- A domain-scoped password token carries secu_admin in its role list and created an agency through POST /v3.0/OS-AGENCY/agencies on the first attempt. For the same identity an AK/SK signed request returned 403 identity:createAgencyEx and a project-scoped password token returned 403. (KB-TR6U, V3 live, checked 2026-07-29)
- A group with Tenant Guest on the region project and on the domain lets a federated identity list VPC, ECS, EVS, IMS, ELB v3, DNS, RDS, CCE clusters, SMN, CES, LTS, CTS and RMS, and every write tried (a VPC create) is refused with 403. (KB-TYWT, V3 live, checked 2026-10-26)
- For the same call, AK/SK, a project-scoped token and a domain-scoped token can return different results. The secu_admin role is carried only by a domain-scoped token. (KB-UDOY, V3 live, checked 2026-07-30)
- On TCP the default IAM quota is 10 identity providers and 10 mappings per tenant. GET /v3.0/OS-QUOTA/domains/{domain_id} returned quota 10 for type idp (max 1000) and type mapping (max 100) on two test tenants in eu-de on 2026-10-02. (KB-UNDV, V3 live, checked 2026-10-02)
- Unbinding a virtual MFA device while login protection with verification_method vmfa is still enabled returns 400 with error code IAM.1062. (KB-V6W3, V3 live, checked 2026-08-19)
- Microsoft: the signing keys of Entra ID roll on a periodic basis with no set or guaranteed time between rolls and in an emergency at once; the discovery document always holds more than one valid key and a background refresh every hour is recommended. With the stored static JWKS of a TCP IAM OpenID Connect identity provider (KB-5CFW) this means a scheduled job must copy the keys of the jwks_uri into the provider configuration. (KB-V7XV, V1 docs, checked 2026-11-21)
- GET /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/credentials/{access_key} returns user_id, status and create_time for one key, and GET /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/credentials?user_id={user_id} lists every key of that user with its description. Both are called with a domain-scoped token in the X-Auth-Token header. (KB-VCXE, V3 live, checked 2026-08-24)
- The authorization engine evaluates IAM-project policies before enterprise-project policies and returns on the first Allow. Effective access is cumulative, so a group holding a tenant-wide OBS grant reaches every bucket regardless of an enterprise-project scoped grant. (KB-VT5H, V1 docs, checked 2026-07-15)
- The OIDC id_token exchange issues a token scoped to project eu-nl even when the mapped group holds no role there. The token carries the eu-nl project and is refused on eu-de endpoints with region mismatch errors (Common.0013 on ECS and IMS). (KB-VV2J, V3 live, checked 2026-10-26)
- A TCP IAM password token scoped to the region project eu-de is refused for an ECS call on the id of another project of the same domain with HTTP 400 Common.0018 (tenantId in token is not the same with in URL); each project needs its own scoped token. (KB-VVXW, V3 live, checked 2026-10-26)
- The id_token to IAM token exchange lives at /v3.0/OS-AUTH/id-token/tokens on eu-de and accepts POST only. A GET on the same path answered HTTP 404 with error code APIGW.0101. The request sends auth.id_token.id, an optional auth.scope.project and the header X-Idp-Id. (KB-W3ZA, V4 kept live, checked 2026-07-02)
- Without X-Project-Id in the signature, temporary AK/SK from method token fail on project APIs, each service with its own error: ECS 400 Common.0018, EVS 403 Common.0018, IMS 400 IMG.0030, ELB v3 400 SYS.0400, RDS 400 DBS.280001, SMN 400, CES 401, LTS 403 domain_null, CTS 403 CTS.0013, VPC 401. DNS zones, RMS and IAM answered 200 without the header. (KB-WAUO, V3 live, checked 2026-10-26)
- POST /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/credentials answers 201 with credential.access and credential.secret, and the secret appears in that response only. A description given at creation is stored and shown again in the credential listing. (KB-WWOG, V3 live, checked 2026-08-24)
- A scoped temporary credential comes from POST /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/securitytokens with method assume_role over an agency created by POST /v3.0/OS-AGENCY/agencies, where a scope block is accepted. Without iam:tokens:assume inside the scope of the calling token the request returns 403. In the resulting credential g:MFAPresent stays false. (KB-WY6E, V3 live, checked 2026-08-19)
- The TMS, EPS and RMS (Config) APIs in eu-de accept only a domain-scoped token. The same requests signed with AK/SK and the same requests carrying a project-scoped token were rejected. (KB-X5D6, V3 live, checked 2026-07-17)
- GET /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/credentials/{access_key} also works with an SDK-HMAC-SHA256 signature made with that same key and no token, and returns the user_id that owns the key. (KB-XPIR, V3 live, checked 2026-09-23)
- On 2026-10-02 the read key and the lab key of a TCP test tenant got HTTP 403 IAM.0002 on GET of the IAM password policy and the login policy. The test keys cannot read the tenant security policy. (KB-XQJ7, V3 live, checked 2026-10-02)
- Temporary AK/SK from POST /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/securitytokens with method token work on the project APIs of VPC, ECS, EVS, IMS, ELB v3, RDS, CCE, SMN, CES, LTS and CTS in eu-de when X-Project-Id is signed, and this holds whether the source federated token was project-scoped, domain-scoped or unscoped. (KB-Y6F5, V3 live, checked 2026-10-26)
- On TCP an IAM sub-project carries the region project as parent_id and a name that starts with the region and an underscore. GET /v3/auth/projects on a test tenant in eu-de on 2026-10-02 listed one such sub-project under the eu-de project. (KB-YDZR, V3 live, checked 2026-10-02)
- Custom policies are created with POST /v3.0/OS-ROLE/roles, type XA for project level and AX for domain level. Attaching works with PUT /v3/projects/{project_id}/groups/{group_id}/roles/{role_id} or the /v3/domains equivalent, both 204. The path /v3.0/OS-ROLE/domains/{domain_id}/groups/{group_id}/roles/{role_id} returns 404 and leaves no assignment behind. (KB-YEPT, V3 live, checked 2026-08-19)
- On TCP the default IAM quota is 50 agencies per tenant. GET /v3.0/OS-QUOTA/domains/{domain_id} returned quota 50, min 10 and max 500 for type agency on two test tenants in eu-de on 2026-10-02. (KB-YFYL, V3 live, checked 2026-10-02)
- DELETE of a federation mapping that an identity provider protocol still references returns 400 "Can't delete mapping which exist assignments with idp". The order that works is the identity provider first. After that delete the mapping was gone from the list. (KB-YWFH, V3 live, checked 2026-10-26)
- An IAM user can hold at most two access keys. At that limit one existing key has to be deleted before POST /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/credentials creates a new one, so a rotation removes the old key first. (KB-ZELI, V3 live, checked 2026-08-24)
- On T Cloud Public (TCP) in eu-de the default enterprise project has id 0 and name default. (KB-ZHAE, V3 live, checked 2026-06-02)
- OBS on TCP ignores OBS system roles that are granted to a user group only on the domain (PUT /v3/domains/{domain_id}/groups/{group_id}/roles/{role_id}): real IAM users and federated identities in that group got 403 AccessDenied for 30 minutes. The same roles granted for all resources (PUT /v3/OS-INHERIT/domains/{domain_id}/groups/{group_id}/roles/{role_id}/inherited_to_projects) worked at once. (KB-ZHEX, V3 live, checked 2026-10-28)
- On TCP the default IAM quota is 20 user groups per tenant. GET /v3.0/OS-QUOTA/domains/{domain_id} returned quota 20, min 10 and max 2000 for type group on two test tenants in eu-de on 2026-10-02. (KB-ZR3C, V3 live, checked 2026-10-02)
- Creating an IAM user with POST /v3/users and its permanent key with POST /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/credentials is done with a domain scoped password token sent in the X-Auth-Token header. That token comes from POST /v3/auth/tokens, and the token response carries the domain id at token.domain.id. (KB-ZS5R, V3 live, checked 2026-06-17)
- GET /v1.0/enterprise-projects/quotas returns 200 and reported a quota of 100 enterprise projects per account in eu-de. (KB-ZTRM, V3 live, checked 2026-06-02)

## mrs (116)

- HetuEngine 2.1.0 on MRS 3.5.0-LTS (Trino 399 base) ships a full Iceberg connector in plugin/hetu-iceberg with iceberg-core, iceberg-api, iceberg-parquet, iceberg-orc and iceberg-hive-metastore 1.4.1, alongside Delta Lake and Hudi connectors and connectors for clickhouse, elasticsearch, hbase, mysql, oracle and postgresql. (KB-22JC, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- POST /web/api/v2/clusters/1/services/{name}/commands/start (also stop and restart) with an empty JSON body returns HTTP 202 and a commandId. A second command issued while one is still running is refused with error code (08-4000031). (KB-2TO7, V3 live, checked 2026-07-28)
- The documentation understates what MRS ships: a grep for iceberg across the whole documentation mirror returns zero hits and the MRS component version table lists Hudi without Iceberg, while the running cluster carries Iceberg runtimes for Spark, HetuEngine and Impala. (KB-2X6J, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- On eu-de the MRS version that carries Iceberg rejects the external_datasources parameter by name, and the MRS version that accepts external_datasources ships no Iceberg jars. A durable external Hive metastore and an interactive SQL engine therefore do not run in one MRS cluster there. (KB-2XCG, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- The MRS cluster manager API rejects a PUT that modifies the built-in admin account. (KB-2ZYC, V3 live, checked 2026-07-30)
- The MRS cluster manager API rejects a description field that contains a comma when creating a role or a user. (KB-4AAX, V3 live, checked 2026-07-30)
- The Trino statement API is asynchronous. A caller that only submits the statement records success at submit time, not at query completion, so the outcome needs a separate polling step. (KB-4CS3, V3 live, checked 2026-07-30)
- On an MRS cluster in normal (non-secure) mode the Ranger admin API answers over HTTP on port 6080 under /service/public/v2/api/ and is reachable from inside the cluster only. A cluster in secure mode serves Ranger on a different port. (KB-4G3S, V3 live, checked 2026-07-30)
- Iceberg tables created through MRS Spark register in the MRS Hive Metastore with table_type = ICEBERG and a metadata_location, with previous_metadata_location advancing per commit. That is the catalog the HetuEngine Iceberg connector consumes. (KB-4G6G, V3 live, checked 2026-07-28)
- Every step of a security-hardened MRS cluster build can be driven through the MRS Manager REST API, so the build is scriptable end to end. (KB-4GLX, V3 live, checked 2026-07-30)
- Impala on MRS reads Iceberg tables, prunes partitions and serves DESCRIBE HISTORY and version time travel. An INSERT into a table holding a timestamptz column returns an error, and Spark writes a TIMESTAMP column as timestamptz. (KB-4JTU, V3 live, checked 2026-07-28)
- A five node MRS cluster created through POST /v2/{project_id}/clusters in eu-de took about 23 minutes from the create call to a usable cluster. (KB-4Q3W, V3 live, checked 2026-07-29)
- The request schema of each MRS Manager API endpoint is readable on the Manager node in /opt/Bigdata/om-server/apache-tomcat-*/webapps/web/WEB-INF/validate/validate-rule-*.xml. Those files list, per endpoint and method, every parameter, whether it is required, and its validation regex. (KB-4QC4, V3 live, checked 2026-07-28)
- MapReduce Service (MRS) is a managed big data platform for building and running big data clusters: core nodes process data and store it in HDFS, and task nodes process data and are added or removed by auto scaling rules. The documentation names the engines ClickHouse, Spark, Flink, Kafka and HBase and compatibility with the open-source components. The Regions and Endpoints page lists the MRS API endpoint in eu-de. (KB-4SRI, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-10-14)
- A Ranger obs policy on MRS that grants to a user returns Obs not support User grants, so grants are made to groups. (KB-4VAD, V3 live, checked 2026-07-30)
- hetu-cli echoes its own java command line, including the --password value in clear text, so the password shows up in the process list and in any captured log. (KB-4VW3, V3 live, checked 2026-07-30)
- GET /web/api/v2/permission/clusters/1/services/{service}/views/{view}/resources needs resource_type and resource_name beside path; path=root alone returns error 12-5000036. With path=root, resource_type=parentQueue and resource_name=root it returns the child queues with their Submit and Admin permissions. (KB-5DZX, V3 live, checked 2026-07-28)
- HetuEngine coordinators read catalogs from the hetumetastore database (catalog.management = HETUENGINE), so a catalog .properties file placed on HSBroker is ignored. The HSConsole DataSourceType enum offers no ICEBERG entry, and registering Iceberg as type HIVE injects hive.allow-* properties the connector rejects. (KB-5FDH, V3 live, checked 2026-07-28)
- Killing the coordinator YARN application leaves the HetuEngine compute instance in state FAULT with 0 nodes, and it does not recover on its own. A rebuild through hetu-cli then gets HTTP 500 from HSBroker because no instance exists. (KB-5KTV, V3 live, checked 2026-07-30)
- An Iceberg data source can still be registered in HetuEngine by repointing an unused type's template in the HSConsole datasource.properties, restarting HetuEngine, then posting to /v1/hsconsole/datasource on port 29880 with a datasourceRequest body. hive.config.resources needs absolute paths, otherwise reads fail with "EndPoint is not set". (KB-5TQ6, V3 live, checked 2026-07-28)
- The MRS create call accepts the default ECS agency field and builds the cluster even when IAM holds no agency under that name yet. (KB-6FHT, V3 live, checked 2026-07-28)
- The HetuEngine data source type field takes an HSConsole enum, which on the date of the check held CARBONDATA, CLICKHOUSE, ELASTICSEARCH, GAUSSDB-A, GBASE, HBASE, HETU_ENGINE, HIVE, HUDI, IOTDB, JDBC, MYSQL and ORACLE. CARBONDATA was rejected on a cluster where that component was not installed. (KB-6MXV, V3 live, checked 2026-07-28)
- The HetuEngine coordinator port changes across service restarts; 29870, 29874 and 29879 were observed on one cluster. The current coordinator URI is published in ZooKeeper under /hetuserver/engine/<tenant-id>/coordinator/<node-uuid> in the field uri. (KB-7EI4, V3 live, checked 2026-07-30)
- MRS node group flavors carry the suffix .linux.mrs, for example m3.2xlarge.8.linux.mrs and c4.4xlarge.4.linux.mrs. (KB-AGYN, V3 live, checked 2026-07-28)
- In the MRS cluster manager v2 API the permission endpoints are /permission/user_roles and /permission/user_groups. The paths /permission/roles and /permission/usergroups both return 404. (KB-AIJX, V3 live, checked 2026-07-30)
- HetuEngine on MRS starts a compute instance only after a Manager role grants Submit and Admin on the YARN queue. The role is created with POST /web/api/v2/permission/user_roles carrying an authModel for the Yarn service and the queue as a childQueue resource. (KB-ANOI, V3 live, checked 2026-07-28)
- Submitting a job to /v2/{project_id}/clusters/{cluster_id}/job-executions returns HTTP 400 with errorCode 12000002 when the submitting IAM user name contains a space. Reading job executions with GET on the same path works with such a user. (KB-AXK3, V3 live, checked 2026-07-30)
- A Ranger obs policy on MRS accepts only the access types read and write. The value list returns error 3022 with the text valid access types=[[read, write]]. (KB-AY4L, V3 live, checked 2026-07-30)
- A shallow Iceberg partition spec on the same 1,152,000 rows produced 96 files of 12,000 rows, 0.3 MB total, 3.1 s to write and 5.5 s to roll up. Point lookups ran at the same speed as on the deep spec, because Iceberg prunes on column statistics as well as on partition paths. (KB-COHI, V3 live, checked 2026-07-28)
- POST /v2/{project_id}/clusters returned 12000002 naming external_datasources when cluster_version was MRS 3.5.0-LTS, and accepted the same field on MRS 3.2.0-LTS.2 in eu-de. (KB-D7FX, V3 live, checked 2026-07-29)
- The body of POST /v1/hsconsole/datasource must be wrapped in a datasourceRequest object; an unwrapped body returns error 400001 reporting that datasourceRequest.datasourcename failed parameter verification. GET /v1/hsconsole/datasource/query lists the registered data sources and DELETE /v1/hsconsole/datasource/{name} removes one. (KB-DMK2, V3 live, checked 2026-07-28)
- POST /v2/{project_id}/clusters in eu-de returned 12000092, Failed to get metadata of version, for cluster_version MRS 3.3.0-LTS.1, a version the API reference listed as current at that time. The same call accepted cluster_version MRS 3.5.0-LTS. (KB-DTTH, V3 live, checked 2026-07-29)
- The HetuEngine HSConsole data source API (POST /v1/hsconsole/datasource) accepts type JDBC for an external relational catalog. The values MYSQL and RDS_MYSQL are rejected with HTTP 400 and the message datasourceRequest.type : Parameter verification failed. (KB-DY4O, V3 live, checked 2026-07-30)
- PUT /permission/users/admin on MRS Manager returns error 12-4000192 about the binding between the password policy and that account. (KB-EBCS, V3 live, checked 2026-07-30)
- On a secured MRS cluster, creating a Ranger policy is refused with 'does not have delegated-admin privilege on given resources', both for the Manager administrator and for a member of the group that the shipped policies grant delegated admin to. (KB-EDGF, V3 live, checked 2026-07-30)
- The HSConsole data source registration is POST /v1/hsconsole/datasource on port 29880, served by a single node of the cluster. A GET on the same path returns HTTP 404. (KB-EJA3, V3 live, checked 2026-07-30)
- On MRS the cluster Manager REST API answers over HTTPS on port 28443 at the Manager floating IP, which is the privateIpFirst field of GET /v1.1/{project_id}/cluster_infos/{cluster_id}. It accepts HTTP basic auth with the Manager administrator account and is reachable from inside the VPC. (KB-FGWH, V3 live, checked 2026-07-28)
- Creating a HetuEngine compute instance needs a YARN queue permission: a Manager role with Submit and Admin on root.default. The CLI message "user does not belong to the specified tenant" is misleading; the HSBroker log states that the user has no permission on default. (KB-FJ2A, V3 live, checked 2026-07-28)
- MRS writes Iceberg data over obs:// into an SSE-KMS encrypted parallel file system without checksum or etag errors. All 14 data objects of the test write carried the KMS key; the unencrypted remainder were zero-byte directory markers, since a parallel file system models real directories. (KB-FN3W, V3 live, checked 2026-07-29)
- The node_size field of POST /v2/{project_id}/clusters takes flavor names ending in .linux.mrs, for example c4.4xlarge.4.linux.mrs. A name ending in .linux.bigdata, the suffix used in documentation examples, returned error 12000048 for an unknown product specification. (KB-FT7O, V3 live, checked 2026-07-28)
- Spark writes an Iceberg TIMESTAMP column as timestamptz, so tables written by Spark are readable by Impala but not writable by it. (KB-FTWV, V3 live, checked 2026-07-28)
- On eu-de MRS ships Iceberg support in its Spark, HetuEngine and Impala components, on the MRS version that carries the Iceberg jars. (KB-GA5U, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- A HetuEngine Iceberg data source takes hive.config.resources as absolute paths, and those files have to exist on every node of the cluster. (KB-GFC4, V3 live, checked 2026-07-28)
- HetuEngine on MRS is a Trino distribution and answers the Trino statement API. A POST to /v1/statement carrying X-Trino-User, X-Trino-Catalog and X-Trino-Schema headers with the SQL as the raw body returns a queued query, and nextUri is polled until the state reaches FINISHED. (KB-GOFP, V3 live, checked 2026-07-30)
- A registered HetuEngine data source is immutable through the API.
