The documented workaround replaces machine-api-provider-openstack with a patched image published in SWR. The deviation affects OpenStack-native tooling in general. (KB-5ZKF, V1 docs, checked 2026-06-08)
- OpenShift is orderable on TCP through the Cloud Create self-service catalogue as self-managed Red Hat OpenShift Container Platform, bring your own subscription, installed UPI on the OpenStack-compatible API. Template versions 4.16.19 and 4.18.30 are offered, further versions on request. (KB-6EFK, V0 recheck due, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- The paths /v1/clusters, /v1/fleets, /v1/permissions and /v1/ccechecks were recorded on the eu-de UCS endpoint ucs.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com. Only /v1/clusters was confirmed by a live call, the other three were noted and not called. (KB-DIDW, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-09-01)
- UCS on T Cloud Public (TCP) is domain-scoped. GET /v1/clusters on the eu-de UCS endpoint answered HTTP 200 with an empty item list when signed with a domain-scoped IAM token, and answered HTTP 401 UCS.01000003 when signed with project-scoped AK/SK. (KB-L6UF, V3 live, checked 2026-09-01)
- A Cloud Create OpenShift deployment builds 3 master nodes, at least 2 workers, a bootstrap node that is deleted after installation and a bastion. It needs about 30 minutes or more until the cluster is ready. (KB-UE2E, V1 docs, checked 2026-06-08)
- UCS carries a stated availability date of 01.09.2026 on T Cloud Public (TCP). A signed call reached its eu-de API, while ordering and consumption of the service were not tested. (KB-X3HG, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-10-14)

## llm-gateway (6)

- SmartChat is the managed RAG product built on the LLM Hub: hosted document knowledge bases queried by browser UI or API with over 50 retrieval settings, isolated spaces per tenant, audit logging, role-based access and enterprise SSO, operated by T-Systems in Germany at a monthly flat rate. Access credentials come from the AIFS team on request. (KB-2BED, V1 docs, checked 2026-10-22)
- The LLM Hub (AI Foundation Services) lists 48 models. The open-source models run on T-Cloud in Germany with data processing in Germany. The proprietary models run on Azure (Sweden, France) or GCP (Europe) with data processing in the EU, except the Gemini models and GPT-6.1 Sol, whose server location and data processing are worldwide. (KB-77QY, V0 recheck due, docs, checked 2026-10-22)
- On T Cloud Public (TCP) the LLM Hub is a subscription product of the Marketplace: the customer subscribes after accepting the seller EULA and the platform terms, creates any number of API keys, each shown once, and sees the token consumption per key in the Marketplace dashboard. The Marketplace guide describes it as model inference hosted in Germany and the EU with open-source and proprietary LLMs behind an OpenAI-compatible API. (KB-GIOM, V1 docs, checked 2026-10-20)
- AI Foundation Services, also called LLM Hub, does not appear in the T Cloud Public (TCP) service description of 09.10.2026, while ModelArts, UCS, Cloud Firewall and KMS do appear. It is contracted as a separate product and is not orderable from the TCP catalog. (KB-KSMV, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-10-14)
- Fine-tuning of LLM Hub models is an optional service on separate order, against additional payment and after a feasibility check: Telekom provides the fine-tuning function with its documentation and supports building and integrating the fine-tuned model. Dedicated model instances, installation in a customer-owned environment and models outside the catalog are optional services of the same kind. (KB-WTDS, V1 docs, checked 2026-10-22)
- The LLM Hub API is OpenAI-compatible at https://llm-server.llmhub.t-systems.net/v2 and covers chat completions, streaming, embeddings, function calling with structured JSON output, speech-to-text models (Whisper) and image models. Its documentation names integrations with LangChain and LlamaIndex. (KB-ZAIQ, V1 docs, checked 2026-10-22)

## vpcep (6)

- A VPC endpoint for an interface endpoint service can get the same IPv4 address in a target project, since the address can be set by hand when the endpoint is created there, which needs a subnet in the target VPC whose CIDR contains that address. (KB-DMY4, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- Rebuilding a workload in another project includes creating the VPC endpoint services and the endpoints again in that project. (KB-ESOO, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- VPC Endpoint (VPCEP) provides private connections from a VPC to VPC endpoint services, which are cloud services such as OBS and DNS or the customer's own private services, with no Elastic IP, NAT gateway or VPN connection needed. On-premises networks attached over VPN or Direct Connect can reach those services through a VPC endpoint as well. The Regions and Endpoints page lists API endpoints for VPCEP in eu-de and eu-nl. (KB-LHEC, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-10-14)
- GET /v1/{project_id}/vpc-endpoint-services/public on eu-de returns total_count 3: com.t-systems.otc.eu-de.apig (interface), com.t-systems.otc.eu-de.dns (interface) and com.t-systems.otc.eu-de.obs (gateway). (KB-MV5K, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-10-14)
- The resource opentelekomcloud_vpcep_endpoint_v1 takes the network id of the subnet in the argument subnet_id. (KB-N5BP, V3 live, checked 2026-09-16)
- On T Cloud Public (TCP) in eu-de VPC endpoints are listed with GET /v1/{project_id}/vpc-endpoints on the VPCEP service endpoint. The response returns them under the key endpoints, each entry carrying id, endpoint_service_name, status, ip and vpc_id. (KB-WQHX, V1 docs, checked 2026-09-25)

## bms (5)

- Bare Metal Server (BMS) is ramped down on TCP. Its user guide and API reference remain in the documentation mirror, so documentation presence is no proof that a service is orderable. (KB-DBAV, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- Bare Metal Server (BMS) is ramped down and no longer orderable in eu-de, while its user guide and API reference remain in the documentation: on 2026-09-25 its three flavors were abandon or sellout in every AZ. The six rows under the bms key of the price API are the Physical ECS row BM General Purpose c7t.28xl and five EVS rows, not BMS flavors. (KB-PYYO, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- BMS bare metal is not orderable in eu-de. The service description revision 09.10.2026 carries no BMS entry and offers bare metal as Physical ECS c7t, with physical c9 and m9 announced from 01.10.2026 on request; on 2026-09-25 the BMS flavor listing marked every AZ of the three returned flavors as abandon or sellout. (KB-UEVJ, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-10-14)
- The service description revision 09.10.2026 offers bare metal as Physical ECS: BM General Purpose c7t.28xl, EU-DE only (section 3.1.1.2 and section 6.2.1.19), and the ECS flavor c7t.28xlarge.4.physical reported eu-de-03(normal) on 2026-09-25. Physical c9 and m9 flavors are announced from 01.10.2026 and offered only on request. (KB-WD2H, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-10-14)
- The opentelekomcloud provider 1.37.8 ships 330 resources. Compared with 1.36.64 it adds 15 resources and drops the two BMS resources compute_bms_server_v2 and compute_bms_tags_v2. (KB-WMZI, V3 live, checked 2026-09-16)

## dws (5)

- In DWS, CREATE EXTERNAL SCHEMA and ALTER EXTERNAL SCHEMA point at a metastore: source_type accepts dli, lakeformation or hive. The syntax is available from DWS 8.3.0. (KB-DB4T, V1 docs, checked 2026-07-29)
- A DWS foreign table on OBS reads ORC, TEXT, JSON, CSV, CarbonData, Parquet and Hudi, and writes ORC and Parquet. Iceberg appears in neither list. (KB-JZIX, V1 docs, checked 2026-07-29)
- Data Warehouse Service (DWS) is a managed online analytical database for searching and analyzing large data sets on a distributed architecture. The documentation states that it supports ANSI/ISO SQL-92, SQL-99 and SQL:2003 syntax and is interoperable with the PostgreSQL, Oracle, Teradata and MySQL ecosystems. The Regions and Endpoints page lists the DWS API endpoint in eu-de. (KB-MQCW, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-10-14)
- The DWS flavor dws.m3.xlarge in eu-de carries minAmount 3 in the price API, so the smallest cluster of that flavor is three nodes that run whether or not a query is issued. (KB-TP5W, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- The TCP service description, revision 09.10.2026, section 6.9.2, prices DWS nodes for EU-DE only and marks every EU-NL price column n.a., so DWS is not offered in eu-nl under that contract revision. (KB-XFT6, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-10-14)

## modelarts (5)

- The ModelArts v2 training job listing is POST /v2/{project_id}/training-job-searches, not a GET. GET /v1/{project_id}/services answers HTTP 200 with a count field and serves, like the flavor and engine GETs, as a cheap liveness probe. (KB-FFU5, V3 live, checked 2026-07-02)
- ModelArts is an end-to-end AI development platform that covers data preprocessing and semi-automatic labeling, distributed training, automated model building and deployment of models on T Cloud Public (TCP). The documentation names the open-source frameworks TensorFlow, PyTorch and MindSpore, dedicated resources and custom images for custom frameworks. The Regions and Endpoints page lists the ModelArts API endpoint in eu-de. (KB-O2UG, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-10-14)
- GET /v1/{project_id}/services/specifications returns 5 inference specifications in eu-de: cpu.2u, high.p3 without a GPU, bm.gpu.v100 with one V100 32GB, high.p2 with an 8GB GPU and gpu.t4u8 with a T4 16GB. Their state moves between normal and sellout: on 2026-09-25 two checks disagreed on cpu.2u and high.p3, while high.p2 and gpu.t4u8 were sellout in every read. Sellout marks a specification the listing returns while no capacity is on offer. (KB-Q6XQ, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-10-20)
- The ModelArts endpoint on eu-de resolves and answers AK/SK signed calls. GET /v2/{project_id}/training-job-flavors returned 3 training flavors on 2026-07-02: modelarts.vm.cpu.2u, modelarts.vm.gpu.v100 with one V100 32GB and modelarts.vm.gpu.4v100 with four V100 32GB, each with max_num 1, so single node training. (KB-TOS5, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- GET /v2/{project_id}/training-job-engines returned 2 preset engines on eu-de on 2026-09-25: pytorch_1.8.0-cuda_10.2-py_3.7-ubuntu_18.04-x86_64 and tensorflow_2.1.0-cuda_10.1-py_3.7-ubuntu_18.04-x86_64. (KB-WMZT, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-09-25)

## monitoring (5)

- A TCP Auto Scaling alarm policy (scaling_policy_type ALARM, operation ADD 1) accepts the alarm_id of a Cloud Eye alarm rule on a custom metric posted with POST /V1.0/{project_id}/metric-data (namespace LAB6EDQ.RDS, period 1, >= 3) and acts on it: the alarm turned to alarm 30 s after the value 3 was posted and the group's desired number went from 0 to 1 with change_reason ALARM. (KB-74PS, V3 live, checked 2026-11-12)
- Of the read calls GET ECS servers, VPC security groups, VPC public IPs, EVS volumes, CTS traces and IMS cloudimages, only the IMS image list was written to Cloud Trace Service (trace listImages with the caller's user and source address), so a read-only observer that lists images is visible to every reader of the tenant's traces. (KB-CLRI, V3 live, checked 2026-11-16)
- GET /v2/{project_id}/fgs/functions/{urn}/statistics/{period} returns arrays for count, fail_count, duration, max_duration, min_duration, concurrency_num, instance_num and reject_count. The accepted periods are 5, 15 and 60. (KB-DE5J, V3 live, checked 2026-07-30)
- The public T-Cloud Status Dashboard at https://status.otc-service.com shows the state of about 98 components by region (EU-DE, EU-NL, Global) with incidents and planned maintenances; its data is readable without a key as JSON: GET /v2/components (id, name, attributes type, category, region) and GET /v2/events or /v2/incidents (type incident, maintenance or info; status, impact, start_date, end_date, components, updates). (KB-GI4M, V3 live, checked 2026-11-06)
- POST /V1.0/{project_id}/alarms on Cloud Eye refuses an alarm rule with alarm_action_enabled true and no alarm_actions with ces.0014 'Some content in message body is not correct'; the same rule with alarm_action_enabled false is accepted, also on a custom metric namespace with period 1. (KB-ZPI2, V3 live, checked 2026-11-12)

## lts (4)

- Log Tank Service (LTS) is a central cloud logging service that stores log data from many sources in one place and forwards it to Object Storage Service or Distributed Message Service for further processing or archiving. The documentation names real-time collection of host and cloud service logs, keyword and fuzzy log search, alarm reporting and a retention period the customer sets, after which logs are deleted unless transferred to OBS for long-term storage. The Regions and Endpoints page lists API endpoints for LTS in eu-de and eu-nl. (KB-3YYI, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-10-14)
- GET /v2/{project_id}/groups on the LTS endpoint answers HTTP 200 with a log_groups array. Each entry carries log_group_id, log_group_name and ttl_in_days. (KB-CRKU, V4 kept live, checked 2026-07-02)
- T Cloud Public (TCP) collects logs centrally in Log Tank Service (LTS): host and cloud service logs are gathered in real time into log groups and streams, searched by keyword, used for alarms, kept for a retention period of 1 to 365 days per log group (KB-XLF6) and transferred to OBS or DMS for longer storage. (KB-MZQU, V1 docs, checked 2026-10-11)
- LTS log group retention is set per group through ttl_in_days and runs from 1 to 365 days, with 30 days as the default. (KB-XLF6, V3 live, checked 2026-07-02)

## cci (3)

- A CCI API that answers on eu-de is not proof that CCI can be ordered for production use. The service description, revision 09.10.2026, lists CCI in section 3.12 among the preview and beta versions for testing purposes only, and the CCI user manual still marks it as beta. (KB-KA35, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-10-14)
- Cloud Container Instance (CCI) is a serverless container engine in preview that runs containers without creating or managing server clusters; pods get CPU, memory and, depending on the pod type, GPU. As a preview version it is meant for testing and Telekom advises against live use (see KB-KA35). The Regions and Endpoints page lists the CCI API endpoint in eu-de. (KB-SNAP, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-10-14)
- On the CCI endpoint the paths / and /healthz answered HTTP 404 with error code APIGW.0101, so only the published API paths are reachable through the gateway. (KB-XNOI, V4 kept live, checked 2026-07-02)

## sfs (3)

- Scalable File Service (SFS) provides scalable shared file storage based on NFSv3 inside the customer's tenant. It is offered as SFS General Purpose with volumes up to 4 PB and as SFS Turbo in Standard, Performance and HPC types (HPC 20 to HPC 1000) from 500 GiB up to 1 PB. The Regions and Endpoints page lists an SFS endpoint in eu-de and an SFS Turbo endpoint in eu-nl. (KB-4I6S, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-10-14)
- Scalable File Service on T Cloud Public (TCP) offers NFSv3 access only. SFS Capacity-Oriented, SFS Turbo and General Purpose file systems expose no SMB or CIFS share, and general purpose file systems cannot be mounted to Windows servers. (KB-7TPW, V1 docs, checked 2026-08-24)
- T Cloud Public (TCP) offers shared file storage over NFS: Scalable File Service (SFS) General Purpose and SFS Turbo file systems are accessed over NFSv3 only, without SMB or CIFS shares (KB-7TPW). (KB-TX7A, V0 recheck due, contract, checked 2026-10-14)

## storage (3)

- In eu-de the SFS Capacity-Oriented create POST /v2/{project_id}/shares on the sfs endpoint answers HTTP 404 APIGW.0101 "The API does not exist: method POST not found", with and without the header X-Openstack-Manila-Api-Version (2.28 and 2.9), while GET /v2/{project_id}/shares on the same endpoint answers 200 with an empty list; GET /v2/{project_id}/types answers 404. (KB-5J3S, V3 live, checked 2026-11-22)
- The SFS user guide of T Cloud Public (TCP) says that SFS Turbo file systems cannot be mounted to Windows ECSs and that SFS General Purpose file systems cannot be mounted to Windows servers; only SFS Capacity-Oriented is mounted on Windows, with the Windows Client for NFS over TCP, hard mounts and mount -o nolock. (KB-EOVB, V1 docs, checked 2026-11-21)
- Between two Windows Server 2022 ECS on s7n.xlarge.2 in one eu-de subnet, an SMB share on an SSD EVS data disk of the file server served a CAD-like tree of 20,000 files of 2 to 50 KB in 200 folders as follows: create about 143 s against about 50 s on the client's own SSD data disk, reading every file once 125 to 132 s against 1 to 2 s locally, one small file read median about 7 ms idle and p95 about 90 ms during a 1 GB copy to the share, the 1 GB copy about 3 to 4 s, and five saves of a 200 MB file with rename to a numbered backup about 4 s. (KB-ZR3G, V3 live, checked 2026-11-22)

## dr (2)

- The German twin-core sites are Magdeburg and Biere in Saxony-Anhalt, about 18 km apart. The Dutch region is near Amsterdam, about 550 km away. A separate Swiss offering exists near Bern. (KB-A2NM, V1 docs, checked 2026-07-09)
- For Windows recovery, starting a prepared stopped ECS took 23 s plus about 43 s per data disk attached sequentially, while rebuilding from a backup took 11 to 19 s for the image plus 149 s to RDP with data disks included. The two meet at about three data disks. (KB-B4LD, V3 live, checked 2026-09-02)

## geminidb (2)

- On 2026-11-19 in eu-de, GET /v3/{project_id}/datastores/redis/versions on the GeminiDB endpoint returned HTTP 500 DBS.280005 Server error. Try again later, while the cassandra and influxdb calls answered in the same second. (KB-F3VO, V3 live, checked 2026-11-19)
- On 2026-11-19 in eu-de, GET /v3/{project_id}/datastores/cassandra/versions on the GeminiDB endpoint returned only 3.11, not the 4.0 named in the GeminiDB user guide introduction, and the same call for influxdb returned an empty versions list. (KB-K7JN, V3 live, checked 2026-11-19)

## linux (2)

- scp to MRS nodes fails with 'subsystem request failed on channel 0' because the sftp subsystem is disabled. Copy files by piping over ssh instead: ssh host "cat > /path" < file. (KB-23NN, V3 live, checked 2026-07-28)
- On MRS nodes, sourcing /opt/Bigdata/client/bigdata_env under set -u aborts the script silently because bigdata_env references unset variables. Use set -o pipefail alone. (KB-CEIK, V3 live, checked 2026-07-28)

## tender (2)

- TCP is listed as certified for ISO/IEC 27001, 27017, 27018 and 27701, plus ISO 9001, 14001, 20000-1, 22301, 50001, 45001 and 22237. (KB-R3TV, V0 recheck due, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- KRITIS status for TCP is a registration with the German federal office for information security, not an issued certificate. (KB-TQFH, V0 recheck due, docs, checked 2026-10-08)

## csbs (1)

- CSBS and VBS reached end of life on 2026-01-01 and still run: both have price rows and live endpoints, and shutdown with data deletion is announced separately. (KB-WQA4, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-10-14)

## identity (1)

- Enterprise projects cover 46 service providers in eu-de on 2026-09-25, among them vpc, ecs, evs, rds, obs, elb, kms, cce, css, dws, gaussdb, waf, CFW, cbr, lts, ces, ims, bms, deh, sfs, dli, er, dds, mrs, vpn and APIG. The hss provider is no longer in the list. (KB-SZLC, V0 recheck due, live, checked 2026-09-25)

## taurusdb (1)

- The TaurusDB engine versions are read with GET /v3/{project_id}/datastores/gaussdb-mysql on the gaussdb-mysql endpoint. The singular path /v3/{project_id}/datastore/gaussdb-mysql answers HTTP 404 APIGW.0101. On 2026-11-19 in eu-de it returned one datastore, name 8.0, version 8.0.22. (KB-VWNM, V3 live, checked 2026-11-19)
